\displaystyle\log\biggl(1+\frac{1}{\sum_{\mathbf{v}_{0}\in\mathcal{V}_{1}}\frac{\tau(\mathbf{v}_{0}^{i},\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i},\mathbf{v}_{t}^{i})}{\tau(\mathbf{v}^{*i},\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i},\mathbf{v}_{t}^{i})}(\bar{R}_{t})^{\bar{\omega}(\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{v}_{t})-\bar{\omega}(\mathbf{v}_{0},\mathbf{v}_{t})}}\biggl)

(114)

Denote
Z_{t}^{i}(\mathbf{v}_{0};\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{v}_{t})=(\bar{R}_{t})^{-(\bar{\omega}_{-i}(\mathbf{v}_{0},\mathbf{v}_{t})-\bar{\omega}_{-i}(\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{v}_{t}))}
where
\bar{\omega}_{-i}(\mathbf{v},\tilde{\mathbf{v}})=\sum_{j=1,j\neq i}^{n}1_{\mathbf{v}^{j}\neq\tilde{\mathbf{v}}^{j}}=\bar{\omega}(\mathbf{v},\tilde{\mathbf{v}})-1_{\mathbf{v}^{i}\neq\mathbf{v}^{i}}
.
Z_{t}^{i}(\mathbf{v}_{0};\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{v}_{t})=(\bar{R}_{t})^{-(\bar{\omega}(\mathbf{v}_{0},\mathbf{v}_{t})-\bar{\omega}(\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{v}_{t}))}\cdot\bar{R}_{t}^{-1_{\mathbf{v}^{*i}\neq\mathbf{v}_{t}^{i}}+1_{(\mathbf{v}_{0})^{i}\neq\mathbf{v}_{t}^{i}}}
. For simplicity, we shorthand
Z_{t}^{i}(\mathbf{v}_{0};\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{v}_{t})
as
Z_{t}^{i}(\mathbf{v}_{0})
. We have

\displaystyle\log\frac{(s+1)\cdot q(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|0}^{i}=\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i},\mathbf{v}_{t|0}=\mathbf{v}_{t})}{s\cdot q(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|1}^{i}=\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i},\mathbf{v}_{t|1}=\mathbf{v}_{t})}

(115)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\log\biggl(1+\frac{(s+1)\cdot q(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|0}^{i}=\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i},\mathbf{v}_{t|0}=\mathbf{v}_{t})-s\cdot q(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|1}^{i}=\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i},\mathbf{v}_{t|1}=\mathbf{v}_{t})}{s\cdot q(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|1}^{i}=\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i},\mathbf{v}_{t|1}=\mathbf{v}_{t})}\biggl)

(116)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\log\biggl(1+\frac{1}{\sum_{\mathbf{v}_{0}\in\mathcal{V}_{1}}\bar{\kappa}(\mathbf{v}^{*i},\mathbf{v}_{0}^{i},\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i},\mathbf{v}_{t}^{i})Z_{t}^{i}(\mathbf{v}_{0})}\biggl)

(117)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\log\biggl(1+\frac{1}{\sum_{\mathbf{v}_{0}\in\mathcal{V}_{1},\mathbf{v}_{0}^{i}=\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i}}\bar{\kappa}(\mathbf{v}^{*i},\mathbf{v}_{0}^{i},\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i},\mathbf{v}_{t}^{i})Z_{t}^{i}(\mathbf{v}_{0})+\sum_{\mathbf{v}_{0}\in\mathcal{V}_{1},\mathbf{v}_{0}^{i}\neq\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i}}\bar{\kappa}(\mathbf{v}^{*i},\mathbf{v}_{0}^{i},\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i},\mathbf{v}_{t}^{i})Z_{t}^{i}(\mathbf{v}_{0})}\biggl)

(118)

Observed that if
\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i}=\mathbf{v}^{*i}
,
\bar{\kappa}(\mathbf{v}^{*i},\mathbf{v}_{0}^{i},\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i},\mathbf{v}_{t}^{i})=1
or
\bar{R}_{t-1}^{-1}
. If
\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i}\neq\mathbf{v}^{*}
,
\bar{\kappa}(\mathbf{v}^{*i},\mathbf{v}_{0}^{i},\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i},\mathbf{v}_{t}^{i})=1
or
\bar{R}_{t-1}
. According to Appendix
L
, we have the following qualities for the maximum and minimum of
\log\frac{(s+1)\cdot q(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|0}^{i}=\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i},\mathbf{v}_{t|0}=\mathbf{v}_{t})}{s\cdot q(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|1}^{i}=\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i},\mathbf{v}_{t|1}=\mathbf{v}_{t})}
.

\displaystyle\max_{\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i}\in[k]}\log\frac{(s+1)\cdot q(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|0}^{i}=\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i},\mathbf{v}_{t|0}=\mathbf{v}_{t})}{s\cdot q(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|1}^{i}=\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i},\mathbf{v}_{t|1}=\mathbf{v}_{t})}=\log\frac{(s+1)\cdot q(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|0}^{i}=\mathbf{v}^{*i},\mathbf{v}_{t|0}=\mathbf{v}_{t})}{s\cdot q(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|1}^{i}=\mathbf{v}^{*i},\mathbf{v}_{t|1}=\mathbf{v}_{t})}

(119)

\displaystyle=\log\biggl(1+\frac{1}{\sum_{\mathbf{v}_{0}\in\mathcal{V}_{1},\mathbf{v}_{0}^{i}=\mathbf{v}^{*i}}Z_{t}^{i}(\mathbf{v}_{0})+\bar{R}_{t-1}^{-1}\sum_{\mathbf{v}_{0}\in\mathcal{V}_{1},\mathbf{v}_{0}^{i}\neq\mathbf{v}^{*i}}Z_{t}^{i}(\mathbf{v}_{0})}\biggl)

(120)

\displaystyle\min_{\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i}\in[k]}\log\frac{(s+1)\cdot q(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|0}^{i}=\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i},\mathbf{v}_{t|0}=\mathbf{v}_{t})}{s\cdot q(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|1}^{i}=\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i},\mathbf{v}_{t|1}=\mathbf{v}_{t})}=\log\frac{(s+1)\cdot q(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|0}^{i}=\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i*},\mathbf{v}_{t|0}=\mathbf{v}_{t})}{s\cdot q(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|1}^{i}=\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i*},\mathbf{v}_{t|1}=\mathbf{v}_{t})}

(121)

\displaystyle=\log\biggl(1+\frac{1}{\bar{R}_{t-1}\sum_{\mathbf{v}_{0}\in\mathcal{V}_{1},\mathbf{v}_{0}^{i}=\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i*}}Z_{t}^{i}(\mathbf{v}_{0})+\sum_{\mathbf{v}_{0}\in\mathcal{V}_{1},\mathbf{v}_{0}^{i}\neq\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i*}}Z_{t}^{i}(\mathbf{v}_{0})}\biggl)

(122)

where
\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i*}=\argmax_{\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i}\in[k]\backslash\{\mathbf{v}^{*i}\}}\sum_{\mathbf{v}_{0}\in\mathcal{V}_{1},\mathbf{v}_{0}^{i}=\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i*}}Z_{t}^{i}(\mathbf{v}_{0})
.

Now, we consider
\max_{\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i}}\log\frac{(s+1)\cdot q(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|0}^{i}=\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i},\mathbf{v}_{t|0})}{s\cdot q(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|1}^{i}=\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i},\mathbf{v}_{t|1})}-\min_{\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i}}\log\frac{(s+1)\cdot q(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|0}^{i}=\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i},\mathbf{v}_{t|0})}{s\cdot q(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|1}^{i}=\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i},\mathbf{v}_{t|1})}
:

\displaystyle\max_{\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i}}\log\frac{(s+1)\cdot q(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|0}^{i}=\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i},\mathbf{v}_{t|0})}{s\cdot q(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|1}^{i}=\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i},\mathbf{v}_{t|1})}-\min_{\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i}}\log\frac{(s+1)\cdot q(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|0}^{i}=\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i},\mathbf{v}_{t|0})}{s\cdot q(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|1}^{i}=\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i},\mathbf{v}_{t|1})}

(123)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\log\biggl(1+\frac{1}{\sum_{\mathbf{v}_{0}\in\mathcal{V}_{1},\mathbf{v}_{0}^{i}=\mathbf{v}^{*i}}Z_{t}^{i}(\mathbf{v}_{0})+\bar{R}_{t-1}^{-1}\sum_{\mathbf{v}_{0}\in\mathcal{V}_{1},\mathbf{v}_{0}^{i}\neq\mathbf{v}^{*i}}Z_{t}^{i}(\mathbf{v}_{0})}\biggl)

\displaystyle-\log\biggl(1+\frac{1}{\bar{R}_{t-1}\sum_{\mathbf{v}_{0}\in\mathcal{V}_{1},\mathbf{v}_{0}^{i}=\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i*}}Z_{t}^{i}(\mathbf{v}_{0})+\sum_{\mathbf{v}_{0}\in\mathcal{V}_{1},\mathbf{v}_{0}^{i}\neq\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i*}}Z_{t}^{i}(\mathbf{v}_{0})}\biggl)

(124)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\log\biggl(\frac{1+\frac{1}{\sum_{\mathbf{v}_{0}\in\mathcal{V}_{1},\mathbf{v}_{0}^{i}=\mathbf{v}^{*i}}Z_{t}^{i}(\mathbf{v}_{0})+\bar{R}_{t-1}^{-1}\sum_{\mathbf{v}_{0}\in\mathcal{V}_{1},\mathbf{v}_{0}^{i}\neq\mathbf{v}^{*i}}Z_{t}^{i}(\mathbf{v}_{0})}}{1+\frac{1}{\bar{R}_{t-1}\sum_{\mathbf{v}_{0}\in\mathcal{V}_{1},\mathbf{v}_{0}^{i}=\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i*}}Z_{t}^{i}(\mathbf{v}_{0})+\sum_{\mathbf{v}_{0}\in\mathcal{V}_{1},\mathbf{v}_{0}^{i}\neq\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i*}}Z_{t}^{i}(\mathbf{v}_{0})}}\biggl)

(125)

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(i)}}{{=}}

\displaystyle\log\biggl(1+\frac{\bar{R}_{t-1}\sum_{\mathbf{v}_{0}^{i}=\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i*}}Z_{t}^{i}(\mathbf{v}_{0})+\sum_{\mathbf{v}_{0}^{i}\neq\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i*}}Z_{t}^{i}(\mathbf{v}_{0})-\sum_{\mathbf{v}_{0}^{i}=\mathbf{v}^{*i}}Z_{t}^{i}(\mathbf{v}_{0})-\bar{R}_{t-1}^{-1}\sum_{\mathbf{v}_{0}^{i}\neq\mathbf{v}^{*i}}Z_{t}^{i}(\mathbf{v}_{0})}{(\sum_{\mathbf{v}_{0}^{i}=\mathbf{v}^{*i}}Z_{t}^{i}(\mathbf{v}_{0})+\bar{R}_{t-1}^{-1}\sum_{\mathbf{v}_{0}^{i}\neq\mathbf{v}^{*i}}Z_{t}^{i}(\mathbf{v}_{0}))(1+\bar{R}_{t-1}\sum_{\mathbf{v}_{0}^{i}=\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i*}}Z_{t}^{i}(\mathbf{v}_{0})+\sum_{\mathbf{v}_{0}^{i}\neq\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i*}}Z_{t}^{i}(\mathbf{v}_{0}))}\biggl)

(126)

where in
(i)
, we have omitted the summation constraint
\mathbf{v}_{0}\in\mathcal{V}_{1}
for brevity.

Further, we split
\sum_{\mathbf{v}_{0}^{i}\neq\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i*}}Z_{t}^{i}(\mathbf{v}_{0}),\sum_{\mathbf{v}_{0}^{i}\neq\mathbf{v}^{*i}}Z_{t}^{i}(\mathbf{v}_{0})
two terms as follows:

\displaystyle\sum_{\mathbf{v}_{0}^{i}\neq\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i*}}Z_{t}^{i}(\mathbf{v}_{0})=\sum_{\mathbf{v}_{0}^{i}=\mathbf{v}^{*i}}Z_{t}^{i}(\mathbf{v}_{0})+\sum_{\mathbf{v}_{0}^{i}\neq\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i*},\mathbf{v}^{*i}}Z_{t}^{i}(\mathbf{v}_{0})

(127)

\displaystyle\sum_{\mathbf{v}_{0}^{i}\neq\mathbf{v}^{*i}}Z_{t}^{i}(\mathbf{v}_{0})=\sum_{\mathbf{v}_{0}^{i}=\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i*}}Z_{t}^{i}(\mathbf{v}_{0})+\sum_{\mathbf{v}_{0}^{i}\neq\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i*},\mathbf{v}^{*i}}Z_{t}^{i}(\mathbf{v}_{0})

(128)

Plugging back the split terms into Eq. (
126
), we get

\displaystyle\max_{\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i}}\log\frac{(s+1)\cdot q(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|0}^{i}=\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i},\mathbf{v}_{t|0})}{s\cdot q(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|1}^{i}=\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i},\mathbf{v}_{t|1})}-\min_{\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i}}\log\frac{(s+1)\cdot q(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|0}^{i}=\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i},\mathbf{v}_{t|0})}{s\cdot q(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|1}^{i}=\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i},\mathbf{v}_{t|1})}

(129)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\log\biggl(1+\frac{(a-1)[(a+1)y+z]}{(ax+y+z)(ay+x+z+1)}\biggl)

(130)

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(i)}}{{\leq}}

\displaystyle\log\biggl(1+\frac{(a-1)(a+1)}{(a^{2}+1)x+ay+az+1}\biggl)

(131)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\log(1+\frac{(\bar{R}_{t-1}-1)(\bar{R}_{t-1}+1)}{(\bar{R}_{t-1}^{2}+1)\sum_{\mathbf{v}_{0}^{i}=\mathbf{v}^{*i}}Z_{t}^{i}(\mathbf{v}_{0})+\bar{R}_{t-1}\sum_{\mathbf{v}_{0}^{i}=\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i*}}Z_{t}^{i}(\mathbf{v}_{0})+\bar{R}_{t-1}\sum_{\mathbf{v}_{0}^{i}\neq\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i*},\mathbf{v}^{*i}}Z_{t}^{i}(\mathbf{v}_{0})+1})

(132)

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(ii)}}{{\leq}}

\displaystyle\log(1+\frac{(\bar{R}_{t-1}-1)(\bar{R}_{t-1}+1)}{(\bar{R}_{t-1}^{2}+1)\cdot\zeta(\mathcal{V}_{1}^{i|\mathbf{v}^{*i}},\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{v}_{t},t)+\bar{R}_{t-1}/\bar{R}_{t}\cdot\zeta(\mathcal{V}_{1}\backslash\mathcal{V}_{1}^{i|\mathbf{v}^{*i}},\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{v}_{t},t)+1})

(133)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\log(1+\frac{(\bar{R}_{t-1}-1)(\bar{R}_{t-1}+1)}{(\bar{R}_{t-1}^{2}+1)\cdot\zeta(\mathcal{V}_{1}^{i|\mathbf{v}^{*i}},\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{v}_{t},t)+\bar{R}_{t-1}/\bar{R}_{t}\cdot\zeta(\mathcal{V}_{1},\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{v}_{t},t)+1})

(134)

where we define
a=\bar{R}_{t-1},x=\sum_{\mathbf{v}_{0}\in\mathcal{V}_{1},\mathbf{v}_{0}^{i}=\mathbf{v}^{*i}}Z_{t}^{i}(\mathbf{v}_{0}),y=\sum_{\mathbf{v}_{0}\in\mathcal{V}_{1},\mathbf{v}_{0}^{i}=\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i*}}Z_{t}^{i}(\mathbf{v}_{0}),z=\sum_{\mathbf{v}_{0}\in\mathcal{V}_{1},\mathbf{v}_{0}^{i}\neq\mathbf{v}^{*i},\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i*}}Z_{t}^{i}(\mathbf{v}_{0})
, and
(i)
is from Lemma
K.5
,
(ii)
is from the definition of
Z_{t}^{i}(\mathbf{v}_{0})
.

Further, we consider
\|q(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|0}^{i}|\mathbf{v}_{t|0}=\mathbf{v}_{t})-q(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|1}^{i}|\mathbf{v}_{t|1}=\mathbf{v}_{t})\|_{l_{1}}

\displaystyle\|q(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|0}^{i}|\mathbf{v}_{t|0}=\mathbf{v}_{t})-q(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|1}^{i}|\mathbf{v}_{t|1}=\mathbf{v}_{t})\|_{l_{1}}

(135)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i}=1}^{k}\biggl|\frac{(\tau(\mathbf{v}^{*i},\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i},\mathbf{v}_{t}^{i})(\bar{R}_{t})^{-\bar{\omega}(\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{v}_{t})})(\sum_{\mathbf{v}_{0}\in\mathcal{V}_{1}}(\bar{R}_{t})^{-\bar{\omega}(\mathbf{v}_{0},\mathbf{v}_{t})})}{(\sum_{\mathbf{v}_{0}\in\mathcal{V}_{1}}(\bar{R}_{t})^{-\bar{\omega}(\mathbf{v}_{0},\mathbf{v}_{t})}))(\sum_{\mathbf{v}_{0}\in\mathcal{V}_{0}}(\bar{R}_{t})^{-\bar{\omega}(\mathbf{v}_{0},\mathbf{v}_{t})}))}

\displaystyle-\frac{((\bar{R}_{t})^{-\bar{\omega}(\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{v}_{t})})(\sum_{\mathbf{v}_{0}\in\mathcal{V}_{1}}\tau(\mathbf{v}_{0}^{i},\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i},\mathbf{v}_{t}^{i})(\bar{R}_{t})^{-\bar{\omega}(\mathbf{v}_{0},\mathbf{v}_{t})})}{(\sum_{\mathbf{v}_{0}\in\mathcal{V}_{1}}(\bar{R}_{t})^{-\bar{\omega}(\mathbf{v}_{0},\mathbf{v}_{t})})(\sum_{\mathbf{v}_{0}\in\mathcal{V}_{0}}(\bar{R}_{t})^{-\bar{\omega}(\mathbf{v}_{0},\mathbf{v}_{t})})}\biggl|

(136)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i}=1}^{k}\biggl|\frac{\sum_{\mathbf{v}_{0}\in\mathcal{V}_{1}}\tau(\mathbf{v}^{*i},\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i},\mathbf{v}_{t}^{i})\cdot(1-\frac{\tau(\mathbf{v}_{0}^{i},\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i},\mathbf{v}_{t}^{i})}{\tau(\mathbf{v}^{*i},\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i},\mathbf{v}_{t}^{i})})(\bar{R}_{t})^{\bar{\omega}(\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{v}_{t})-\bar{\omega}(\mathbf{v}_{0},\mathbf{v}_{t})}}{(\sum_{\mathbf{v}_{0}\in\mathcal{V}_{1}}(\bar{R}_{t})^{\bar{\omega}(\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{v}_{t})-\bar{\omega}(\mathbf{v}_{0},\mathbf{v}_{t})})(1+\sum_{\mathbf{v}_{0}\in\mathcal{V}_{1}}(\bar{R}_{t})^{\bar{\omega}(\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{v}_{t})-\bar{\omega}(\mathbf{v}_{0},\mathbf{v}_{t})})}\biggl|

(137)

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(i)}}{{\leq}}

\displaystyle\mu_{t}^{+}\cdot(\frac{\bar{\mu}_{t-1}^{+}}{\bar{\mu}_{t}^{+}}-\frac{\bar{\mu}_{t-1}^{-}}{\bar{\mu}_{t}^{-}})\cdot\frac{1}{1+\sum_{\mathbf{v}_{0}\in\mathcal{V}_{1}}(\bar{R}_{t})^{\bar{\omega}(\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{v}_{t})-\bar{\omega}(\mathbf{v}_{0},\mathbf{v}_{t})}}

(138)

where
(i)
is from Lemma
K.6
.

Therefore, summarizing the above, we obtain

\displaystyle\mathcal{D}_{\text{KL}}(q(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|0}^{i}|\mathbf{v}_{t|0})\|q(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|1}^{i}|\mathbf{v}_{t|1}))+\mathcal{D}_{\text{KL}}(q(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|1}^{i}|\mathbf{v}_{t|1})\|q(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|0}^{i}|\mathbf{v}_{t|0}))

(139)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\|q(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|0}^{i}=\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i}|\mathbf{v}_{t|0})-q(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|1}^{i}=\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i}|\mathbf{v}_{t|1})\|_{l_{1}}\biggl[\max_{\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i}}\log\frac{q(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|0}^{i}=\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i},\mathbf{v}_{t|0})}{q(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|1}^{i}=\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i},\mathbf{v}_{t|1})}

\displaystyle-\min_{\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i}}\log\frac{q(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|0}^{i}=\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i},\mathbf{v}_{t|0})}{q(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|1}^{i}=\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i},\mathbf{v}_{t|1})}\biggl]

(140)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{\mu_{t}^{+}\cdot(\frac{\bar{\mu}_{t-1}^{+}}{\bar{\mu}_{t}^{+}}-\frac{\bar{\mu}_{t-1}^{-}}{\bar{\mu}_{t}^{-}})}{1+\zeta(\mathcal{V}_{1},\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{v}_{t},t)}\cdot\log\biggl(1+\frac{(\bar{R}_{t-1}-1)(\bar{R}_{t-1}+1)}{(\bar{R}_{t-1}^{2}+1)\cdot\zeta(\mathcal{V}_{1}^{i|\mathbf{v}^{*i}},\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{v}_{t},t)+\bar{R}_{t-1}/\bar{R}_{t}\cdot\zeta(\mathcal{V}_{1},\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{v}_{t},t)+1}\biggl)

(141)

Define
\Delta_{t}^{i}(\cdot)
as:

\displaystyle\Delta_{t}^{i}(\mathbf{v}_{t})=\frac{\mu_{t}^{+}\cdot(\frac{\bar{\mu}_{t-1}^{+}}{\bar{\mu}_{t}^{+}}-\frac{\bar{\mu}_{t-1}^{-}}{\bar{\mu}_{t}^{-}})}{1+\zeta(\mathcal{V}_{1},\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{v}_{t},t)}\cdot\log\biggl(1+\frac{(\bar{R}_{t-1}-1)(\bar{R}_{t-1}+1)}{(\bar{R}_{t-1}^{2}+1)\cdot\zeta(\mathcal{V}_{1}^{i|\mathbf{v}^{*i}},\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{v}_{t},t)+\bar{R}_{t-1}/\bar{R}_{t}\cdot\zeta(\mathcal{V}_{1},\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{v}_{t},t)+1}\biggl)

(142)

We consider a upper bound on
\sum_{i=1}^{n}\Delta_{t}^{i}
. Let
C_{t}=\frac{\mu_{t}^{+}\cdot(\frac{\bar{\mu}_{t-1}^{+}}{\bar{\mu}_{t}^{+}}-\frac{\bar{\mu}_{t-1}^{-}}{\bar{\mu}_{t}^{-}})}{1+\zeta(\mathcal{V}_{1},\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{v}_{t},t)}
, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{n}\Delta_{t}^{i}

\displaystyle=C_{t}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\log\biggl(1+\frac{(\bar{R}_{t-1}-1)(\bar{R}_{t-1}+1)}{(\bar{R}_{t-1}^{2}+1)\cdot\zeta(\mathcal{V}_{1}^{i|\mathbf{v}^{*i}},\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{v}_{t},t)+\bar{R}_{t-1}/\bar{R}_{t}\cdot\zeta(\mathcal{V}_{1},\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{v}_{t},t)+1}\biggl)

(143)

\displaystyle=:\Delta_{t}

(144)

From above, we have,

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathcal{D}_{\text{KL}}(q(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|0}^{i}|\mathbf{v}_{t|0})\|q(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|1}^{i}|\mathbf{v}_{t|1}))+\mathcal{D}_{\text{KL}}(q(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|1}^{i}|\mathbf{v}_{t|1})\|q(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|0}^{i}|\mathbf{v}_{t|0}))\leq\Delta_{t}(\mathbf{v}_{t})

(145)

∎

J.5
Proof of Lemma
A.5

According to measure partition, we have split measure
q(\mathbf{v}_{t|0})
into
q(\mathbf{v}_{t}\in\mathcal{S}_{a}),q(\mathbf{v}_{t}\in\mathcal{S}_{b})
and
q(\mathbf{v}_{t}\in\mathcal{S}_{c})
. Therefore,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{v}_{t}\sim q(\mathbf{v}_{t|0})}[\Delta_{t}(\mathbf{v}_{t})]=q(\mathbf{v}_{t}\in\mathcal{S}_{a})\cdot\Delta_{t}(\mathbf{v}_{t})+q(\mathbf{v}_{t}\in\mathcal{S}_{b})\cdot\Delta_{t}(\mathbf{v}_{t})+q(\mathbf{v}_{t}\in\mathcal{S}_{c})\cdot\Delta_{t}(\mathbf{v}_{t})

(146)

Now, we consider the behavior of
\Delta_{t}(\cdot)
with respect to
\mathbf{v}_{t}
for each measure.
For measure
q(\mathbf{v}_{t}\in\mathcal{S}_{a})
and
q(\mathbf{v}_{t}\in\mathcal{S}_{c})
, since
\zeta(\mathcal{V}_{1},\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{v}_{t},t)\geq\text{Sim}(\mathcal{V}_{1},\mathbf{v}^{*},t)
, we have

\displaystyle\Delta_{t}(\mathbf{v}_{t})\leq

\displaystyle\frac{\mu_{t}^{+}(\bar{\mu}_{t-1}^{+}/\bar{\mu}_{t}^{+}-\bar{\mu}_{t-1}^{-}/\bar{\mu}_{t}^{-})}{1+\text{Sim}(\mathcal{V}_{1},\mathbf{v}^{*},t)}\cdot\sum_{i=1}^{n}\log(1+\frac{\bar{R}_{t-1}^{2}-1}{\bar{R}_{t-1}^{2}\cdot\text{Sim}(\mathcal{V}_{1}^{i|\mathbf{v}^{*i}},\mathbf{v}^{*},t)+\text{Sim}(\mathcal{V}_{1},\mathbf{v}^{*},t)+1})

(147)

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{def}}}{{=:}}

\displaystyle\frac{n}{s^{\psi_{t}}}

(148)

where
\mathcal{A}_{t}=\mu_{t}^{+}\cdot(\bar{\mu}_{t-1}^{+}/\bar{\mu}_{t}^{+}-\bar{\mu}_{t-1}^{-}/\bar{\mu}_{t}^{-})
and
\mathcal{B}_{t}=\bar{R}_{t-1}^{2}-1
. Here, we use notation
\frac{1}{s^{\psi_{t}}}
instead of a single symbol is to clearly show how the order of privacy leakage varies with respect to
t
in generation process.
For measure
q(\mathbf{v}_{t}\in\mathcal{S}_{b})
, we have much smaller privacy leakage than
q(\mathbf{v}_{t}\in\mathcal{S}_{a})
and
q(\mathbf{v}_{t}\in\mathcal{S}_{c})
. When the
\text{supp}(\mathcal{V}_{0})=\text{supp}(\mathcal{V}_{1})
,
\mathcal{V}_{1}^{i|\mathbf{v}^{*i}}\neq\emptyset
, we have

\displaystyle\Delta_{t}(\mathbf{v}_{t})\leq

\displaystyle\frac{\mathcal{A}_{t}}{1+\bar{R}_{t}^{\eta_{t}^{\prime}}}\cdot\sum_{i=1}^{n}\log\biggl(1+\frac{\mathcal{B}_{t}}{1+\bar{R}_{t-1}^{2}\cdot\bar{R}_{t}^{\eta_{t}^{\prime}}}\biggl)

(149)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{n\cdot\mathcal{A}_{t}\cdot\mathcal{B}_{t}}{(1+\bar{R}_{t}^{\eta_{t}^{\prime}})(1+\bar{R}_{t-1}\cdot\bar{R}_{t}^{\eta_{t}^{\prime}})}\leq\frac{n\cdot\mathcal{A}_{t}\cdot\mathcal{B}_{t}}{\bar{R}_{t-1}^{2}\cdot\bar{R}_{t}^{2\eta_{t}^{\prime}}}

(150)

Therefore,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{v}_{t}}[\Delta_{t}(\mathbf{v}_{t})]\leq q(\mathbf{v}_{t}\in\mathcal{S}_{a})\cdot\frac{n}{s^{\psi_{t}}}+q(\mathbf{v}_{t}\in\mathcal{S}_{b})\cdot\frac{n\cdot\mathcal{A}_{t}\cdot\mathcal{B}_{t}}{\bar{R}_{t-1}^{2}\cdot\bar{R}_{t}^{2\eta_{t}^{\prime}}}+q(\mathbf{v}_{t}\in\mathcal{S}_{c})\cdot\frac{n}{s^{\psi_{t}}}

(151)

Now, based on the above partition, we further estimate the measure of
q(\mathbf{v}_{t}\in\mathcal{S}_{a}),q(\mathbf{v}_{t}\in\mathcal{S}_{b})
and
q(\mathbf{v}_{t}\in\mathcal{S}_{c})
. Recall that
N_{\eta_{t}}(\mathbf{v}^{*})=|\{\mathbf{v}\in\mathcal{V}_{1}\text{ s.t. }\bar{\omega}(\mathbf{v},\mathbf{v}^{*})\leq\eta_{t}\}|
. We have the following:

\displaystyle q(\mathbf{v}_{t}\in\mathcal{S}_{b})\leq

\displaystyle q(\mathbf{v}_{t};(1)\mathbf{v}_{t}\text{ is diffused from }\mathbf{v}_{0}\in\mathcal{V}_{0}\text{ s.t. }\bar{\omega}(\mathbf{v}^{\prime},\mathbf{v}^{*})>\eta_{t})

(152)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{s-N_{\eta_{t}}(\mathbf{v}^{*})}{s}

(153)

\displaystyle q(\mathbf{v}_{t}\in\mathcal{S}_{c})\leq

\displaystyle q(\mathbf{v}_{t};(1)\mathbf{v}_{t}\text{ is diffused from }\mathbf{v}_{0}\in\mathcal{V}_{0}\text{ s.t }\bar{\omega}(\mathbf{v}_{0},\mathbf{v}^{*})\geq\eta_{t}+1\text{ and}

\displaystyle(2)\forall\mathbf{v}_{0}^{\prime}\in\mathcal{V}_{0},\bar{\omega}(\mathbf{v}_{t},\mathbf{v}_{0}^{\prime})\geq\eta_{t}-\eta_{t}^{\prime}+2)

(154)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle q(\mathbf{v}_{t};\mathbf{v}_{t}\text{ is diffused from }\mathbf{v}_{0}\in\mathcal{V}_{0}\text{ s.t. }\bar{\omega}(\mathbf{v}_{0},\mathbf{v}^{*})\geq\eta_{t}+1,\bar{\omega}(\mathbf{v}_{t},\mathbf{v}_{0})\geq\eta_{t}-\eta_{t}^{\prime}+2)

(155)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{s-N_{\eta_{t}}(\mathbf{v}^{*})}{s}\cdot\min\{(n(1-\bar{\mu}_{t}^{+}))^{\eta_{t}-\eta_{t}^{\prime}+2},1\}

(156)

As for
q(\mathbf{v}_{t}\in\mathcal{S}_{a})
, we need further characterization. Let
c_{t}^{*}
be a tunable positive constant and we have the following:

\displaystyle q(\mathbf{v}_{t}\in\mathcal{S}_{a})\leq

\displaystyle q(\mathbf{v}_{t};\mathbf{v}_{t}\text{ is diffused from }\mathbf{v}_{0}\in\mathcal{V}_{0}\text{ s.t. }\bar{\omega}(\mathbf{v}_{0},\mathbf{v}^{*})\leq\eta_{t})

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle q(\mathbf{v}_{t};\mathbf{v}_{t}\text{ is diffused from }\mathbf{v}_{0}\in\mathcal{V}_{0}\text{ s.t. }\bar{\omega}(\mathbf{v}_{0},\mathbf{v}^{*})\in[\eta_{t}+1,(1+c_{t}^{*})\eta_{t}],\bar{\omega}(\mathbf{v}_{t},\mathbf{v}^{*})\leq\eta_{t})

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle q(\mathbf{v}_{t};\mathbf{v}_{t}\text{ is diffused from }\mathbf{v}_{0}\in\mathcal{V}_{0}\text{ s.t. }\bar{\omega}(\mathbf{v}_{0},\mathbf{v}^{*})\geq(1+c_{t}^{*})\eta_{t}+1,\bar{\omega}(\mathbf{v}_{t},\mathbf{v}^{*})\leq\eta_{t})

(157)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\underbrace{\frac{N_{\eta_{t}}(\mathbf{v}^{*})}{s}+\sum_{\eta=\eta_{t}+1}^{(1+c_{t}^{*})\eta_{t}}\frac{\Delta N_{\eta}(\mathbf{v}^{*})}{s}\tbinom{n}{\eta-\eta_{t}}(\bar{\mu}_{t}^{-})^{\eta-\eta_{t}}}_{\text{merge together}}+\frac{s-N_{(1+c_{t}^{*})\eta_{t}}(\mathbf{v}^{*})}{s}\max_{k;k\geq\eta_{t}}\tbinom{k}{k-\eta_{t}}(\bar{\mu}_{t}^{-})^{k-\eta_{t}}

(158)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{N_{(1+c_{t}^{*})\eta_{t}}(\mathbf{v}^{*})}{s}+\frac{s-N_{(1+c_{t}^{*})\eta_{t}}(\mathbf{v}^{*})}{s}\max_{h;h\geq(1+c_{t}^{*})\eta_{t}}\tbinom{h}{h-\eta_{t}}(\bar{\mu}_{t}^{-})^{h-\eta_{t}}

(159)

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(i)}}{{\leq}}

\displaystyle\frac{N_{(1+c_{t}^{*})\eta_{t}}(\mathbf{v}^{*})}{s}+\frac{s-N_{(1+c_{t}^{*})\eta_{t}}(\mathbf{v}^{*})}{s}\min\max_{h;h\geq(1+c_{t}^{*})\eta_{t}}\biggl\{\biggl[\frac{heq_{t}}{h-\eta_{t}}\biggl]^{h-\eta_{t}},\biggl[\frac{he}{\eta_{t}}\biggl]^{\eta_{t}}q_{t}^{h-\eta_{t}}\biggl\}

(160)

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(ii)}}{{\leq}}

\displaystyle\frac{N_{(1+c_{t}^{*})\eta_{t}}(\mathbf{v}^{*})}{s}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{s-N_{(1+c_{t}^{*})\eta_{t}}(\mathbf{v}^{*})}{s}\biggl[((1+c_{t}^{*})e)^{\eta_{t}}(\bar{\mu}_{t}^{-})^{c_{t}^{*}\eta_{t}}\mathbbm{1}_{c_{t}^{*}<1}+(\frac{(1+c_{t}^{*})e}{c_{t}^{*}})^{c_{t}^{*}\eta_{t}}(\bar{\mu}_{t}^{-})^{c_{t}^{*}\eta_{t}}\mathbbm{1}_{c_{t}^{*}\geq 1}\biggl]

(161)

(i)
is from
\tbinom{n}{k}\leq(\frac{en}{k})^{k}
and
(ii)
is from Corollary
K.1
where we need
e\bar{\mu}_{t}^{-}<\frac{4}{5}
(i.e.
k\geq 3
).

Now, we want further balance the two terms
\frac{N_{(1+c_{t}^{*})\eta_{t}}(\mathbf{v}^{*})}{s}
and
\frac{s-N_{(1+c_{t}^{*})\eta_{t}}(\mathbf{v}^{*})}{s}\biggl[((1+c_{t}^{*})e)^{\eta_{t}}(\bar{\mu}_{t}^{-})^{c_{t}^{*}\eta_{t}}\mathbbm{1}_{c_{t}^{*}<1}+(\frac{(1+c_{t}^{*})e}{c_{t}^{*}})^{c_{t}^{*}\eta_{t}}(\bar{\mu}_{t}^{-})^{c_{t}^{*}\eta_{t}}\mathbbm{1}_{c_{t}^{*}\geq 1}\biggl]
, i.e. balancing
\frac{N_{(1+c_{t}^{*})\eta_{t}}(\mathbf{v}^{*})}{s-N_{(1+c_{t}^{*})\eta_{t}}(\mathbf{v}^{*})}
and
\biggl[((1+c_{t}^{*})e)^{\eta}_{t}(\bar{\mu}_{t}^{-})^{c_{t}^{*}\eta_{t}}\mathbbm{1}_{c_{t}^{*}<1}+(\frac{(1+c_{t}^{*})e}{c_{t}^{*}})^{c_{t}^{*}\eta_{t}}(\bar{\mu}_{t}^{-})^{c_{t}^{*}\eta_{t}}\mathbbm{1}_{c_{t}^{*}\geq 1}\biggl]
. First, compare
\frac{N_{2\eta_{t}}(\mathbf{v}^{*})}{s-N_{2\eta_{t}}(\mathbf{v}^{*})}
and
(2e\bar{\mu}_{t}^{-})^{\eta_{t}}
.

(1) If
\frac{N_{2\eta_{t}}(\mathbf{v}^{*})}{s-N_{2\eta_{t}}(\mathbf{v}^{*})}>(2e\bar{\mu}_{t}^{-})^{\eta_{t}}
, we can find a smaller
c_{t}^{*}
to make the bound Eq. (
161
) tight. Instead of working directly on
((1+c_{t}^{*})e)^{\eta_{t}}(\bar{\mu}_{t}^{-})^{c_{t}^{*}\eta_{t}}\leq\frac{N_{(1+c_{t}^{*})\eta_{t}}(\mathbf{v}^{*})}{s-N_{(1+c_{t}^{*})\eta_{t}}(\mathbf{v}^{*})}
, we consider a sufficient condition: found smallest
c_{t}^{*}
such that

\displaystyle(2e)^{\eta_{t}}(\bar{\mu}_{t}^{-})^{c_{t}^{*}\eta_{t}}\leq\frac{N_{(1+c_{t}^{*})\eta_{t}}(\mathbf{v}^{*})}{s-N_{(1+c_{t}^{*})\eta_{t}}(\mathbf{v}^{*})}

(162)

i.e.

\displaystyle c_{t}^{*}\geq\frac{\frac{1}{\eta_{t}}\log\biggl(\frac{s-N_{(1+c_{t}^{*})\eta_{t}}(\mathbf{v}^{*})}{N_{(1+c_{t}^{*})\eta_{t}}(\mathbf{v}^{*})}\biggl)+1+\log 2}{\log\frac{1}{\bar{\mu}_{t}^{-}}}

(163)

Select the smallest
c_{t}^{*}
that satisfies Eq. (
163
). Since
(1+c_{t}^{*})\eta_{t}\in\{0,1,2,...,n\}
. Let
\eta_{t}^{*}=\lceil(1+c^{*})\eta_{t}\rceil
.

(2) If
\frac{N_{2\eta_{t}}(\mathbf{v}^{*})}{s-N_{2\eta_{t}}(\mathbf{v}^{*})}\leq(2e\mu_{t}^{-})^{\eta_{t}}
, we want to find the smallest
c
such that
(\frac{(1+c_{t}^{*})e}{c_{t}^{*}})^{c_{t}^{*}\eta_{t}}(\mu_{t}^{-})^{c_{t}^{*}\eta_{t}}\leq\frac{N_{(1+c_{t}^{*})\eta_{t}}(\mathbf{v}^{*})}{s-N_{(1+c_{t}^{*})\eta_{t}}(\mathbf{v}^{*})}
. Similar, since
(\frac{1+c_{t}^{*}}{c_{t}^{*}})^{c_{t}^{*}}\leq e
, we also consider a sufficient condition: found smallest
c_{t}^{*}
such that

\displaystyle e^{\eta_{t}}(e\mu_{t}^{-})^{c_{t}^{*}\eta_{t}}\leq\frac{N_{(1+c_{t}^{*})\eta_{t}}(\mathbf{v}^{*})}{s-N_{(1+c_{t}^{*})\eta_{t}}(\mathbf{v}^{*})}

(164)

i.e.

\displaystyle c_{t}^{*}\geq\frac{\frac{1}{\eta_{t}}\log\biggl(\frac{s-N_{(1+c_{t}^{*})\eta_{t}}(\mathbf{v}^{*})}{N_{(1+c_{t}^{*})\eta_{t}}(\mathbf{v}^{*})}\biggl)}{\log\frac{1}{\bar{\mu}_{t}^{-}}-1}

(165)

Select the smallest
c_{t}^{*}
that satisfies Eq. (
165
).

The above procedure for finding
(1+c_{t}^{*})\eta_{t}
can be described in Algorithm
.
After finding the most suitable
(1+c_{t}^{*})\eta_{t}
,
q(\mathbf{v}_{t}\in\mathcal{S}_{a})
can be upper bounded by

\displaystyle q(\mathbf{v}_{t}\in\mathcal{S}_{a})\leq

\displaystyle\frac{2N_{(1+c_{t}^{*})\eta_{t}}(\mathbf{v}^{*})}{s}

(166)

Now, we examine the selection of
\eta_{t}^{\prime}
. Our aim is to let
q(\mathbf{v}_{t}\in\mathcal{S}_{b})\cdot\frac{n\cdot\mathcal{A}_{t}\cdot\mathcal{B}_{t}}{\bar{R}_{t-1}^{2}\cdot\bar{R}_{t}^{2\eta_{t}^{\prime}}}
be dominated by
\frac{N_{(1+c_{t}^{*})\eta_{t}}(\mathbf{v}^{*})}{s}\cdot\frac{n}{s^{\psi_{t}}}
. Consider the following sufficient condition:

\displaystyle\frac{N_{(1+c_{t}^{*})\eta_{t}}(\mathbf{v}^{*})}{s}\cdot\frac{n}{s^{\psi_{t}}}\geq\frac{s-N_{\eta_{t}}(\mathbf{v}^{*})}{s}\cdot\frac{n\cdot\mathcal{A}_{t}\cdot\mathcal{B}_{t}}{\bar{R}_{t-1}^{2}\cdot\bar{R}_{t}^{2\eta_{t}^{\prime}}}

(167)

i.e.

\displaystyle\eta_{t}^{\prime}\geq\max\left\{\frac{\log\frac{s-N_{\eta_{t}}(\mathbf{v}^{*})}{N_{(1+c_{t}^{*})\eta_{t}}(\mathbf{v}^{*})}+\log(\mathcal{A}_{t}\cdot\mathcal{C}_{t}\cdot s^{\psi_{t}})}{2\log\bar{R}_{t}},0\right\}

(168)

where
\mathcal{C}_{t}=\mathcal{B}_{t}/\bar{R}_{t-1}^{2}=1-1/\bar{R}_{t-1}^{2}
.

Note that the r.h.s is monotonically decreasing w.r.t
\eta_{t}
. However, since
\eta_{t}^{\prime}\in\{0,1,2,...,n\}
, we have to make sure the r.h.s is dominated by
n
. In fact, this constraint is naturally satisfied as
\log(\frac{s-N_{\eta_{t}}(\mathbf{v}^{*})}{N_{(1+c_{t}^{*})\eta_{t}}(\mathbf{v}^{*})+1})
goes to
-\infty
when
\eta_{t}\to n
.

Finally, we control the third term,
\frac{s-N_{\eta_{t}}(\mathbf{v}^{*})}{s}\min\{(n(1-\bar{\mu}_{t}^{+}))^{\eta_{t}-\eta_{t}^{\prime}+2},1\}\cdot\frac{n}{s^{\psi_{t}}}
1
1

1

Instead of using Eq.
156
, a better bound to estimate the third term is
\frac{s-N_{\eta_{t}}(\mathbf{v}^{*})}{s}\cdot\mathbb{P}(\bar{\omega}(\mathbf{v}_{t},\mathbf{v}_{0})\geq\eta_{t}-\eta_{t}^{\prime}+2)
and trade-off with first term, we get the smallest
\eta_{t}-\eta_{t}^{\prime}+2
such that
\mathbb{P}(\bar{\omega}(\mathbf{v}_{t},\mathbf{v}_{0})\geq\eta_{t}-\eta_{t}^{\prime}+2)\leq\frac{N_{(1+c_{t}^{*})\eta_{t}}(\mathbf{v}^{*})}{s-N_{\eta_{t}}(\mathbf{v}^{*})}
where
\mathbb{P}(\bar{\omega}(\mathbf{v}_{t},\mathbf{v}_{0})\geq\eta_{t}-\eta_{t}^{\prime}+2)=\sum_{j=\eta_{t}-\eta_{t}^{\prime}+2}^{n}\binom{n}{j}(1-\bar{\mu}_{t}^{+})^{j}(\bar{\mu}_{t}^{+})^{n-j}
.
to be dominated be the first term
\frac{N_{(1+c_{t}^{*})\eta_{t}}(\mathbf{v}^{*})}{s}\cdot\frac{n}{s^{\psi_{t}}}
. That is

\displaystyle\frac{N_{(1+c_{t}^{*})\eta_{t}}(\mathbf{v}^{*})}{s}\geq\frac{s-N_{\eta_{t}}(\mathbf{v}^{*})}{s}\min\{(n(1-\bar{\mu}_{t}^{+}))^{\eta_{t}-\eta_{t}^{\prime}+2},1\}

(169)

i.e.

\displaystyle\frac{N_{(1+c_{t}^{*})\eta_{t}}(\mathbf{v}^{*})}{s}\geq\frac{s-N_{\eta_{t}}(\mathbf{v}^{*})}{s}(\min\{n(1-\bar{\mu}_{t}^{+}),1\})^{\eta_{t}-\eta_{t}^{\prime}+2}

(170)

\displaystyle\eta_{t}-\eta_{t}^{\prime}+2\geq\frac{\log\frac{s-N_{\eta_{t}}(\mathbf{v}^{*})}{N_{(1+c_{t}^{*})\eta_{t}}(\mathbf{v}^{*})}}{\log\frac{1}{\min\{n(1-\bar{\mu}_{t}^{+}),1\}}}

(171)

Similarly,
\log(\frac{s-N_{\eta_{t}}(\mathbf{v}^{*})}{N_{\eta_{t}^{*}}(\mathbf{v}^{*})+1})
can goes to
-\infty
when
\eta_{t}\to n
and the above inequality naturally holds.
Thus, we combine the above results, we have when
\eta_{t}\in[n]
satisfies:

\displaystyle\eta_{t}\geq\frac{\log\frac{s-N_{\eta_{t}}(\mathbf{v}^{*})}{N_{(1+c_{t}^{*})\eta_{t}}(\mathbf{v}^{*})}}{\log\frac{1}{n(1-\bar{\mu}_{t}^{+})}}+\max\left\{\frac{\log\frac{s-N_{\eta_{t}}(\mathbf{v}^{*})}{N_{(1+c_{t}^{*})\eta_{t}}(\mathbf{v}^{*})}+\log(\mathcal{A}_{t}\cdot\mathcal{C}_{t}\cdot s^{\psi_{t}})}{2\log\bar{R}_{t}},0\right\}-2

(172)

Summarizing the above results, we obtain that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{v}_{t}\sim q(\mathbf{v}_{t|0})}[\Delta_{t}(\mathbf{v}_{t})]\leq

\displaystyle q(\mathbf{v}_{t}\in\mathcal{S}_{a})\cdot\frac{n}{s^{\psi_{t}}}+q(\mathbf{v}_{t}\in\mathcal{S}_{b})\cdot\frac{n\cdot\mathcal{A}_{t}\cdot\mathcal{B}_{t}}{\bar{R}_{t-1}^{2}\cdot\bar{R}_{t}^{2\eta_{t}^{\prime}}}+q(\mathbf{v}_{t}\in\mathcal{S}_{c})\cdot\frac{n}{s^{\psi_{t}}}

(173)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\min\biggl\{\frac{4N_{(1+c_{t}^{*})\eta_{t}}(\mathbf{v}^{*})}{s},1\biggl\}\cdot\frac{n}{s^{\psi_{t}}}

(174)

Summarizing the above results, we calculate the privacy bound of discrete diffusion model by following Algorithm
.

J.6
Proof of Lemma
A.6

Proof.

To begin with,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{v}_{t}\sim(q(\mathbf{v}_{t|1})-q(\mathbf{v}_{t|0}))}[\mathcal{D}_{\text{KL}}(q(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|1}^{i}|\mathbf{v}_{t|1}=\mathbf{v}_{t})\|q(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|0}^{i}|\mathbf{v}_{t|0}=\mathbf{v}_{t}))]

(175)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{v}_{t}\sim q(\mathbf{v}_{t|0})}\biggl[\biggl(1-\frac{q(\mathbf{v}_{t|0}=\mathbf{v}_{t})}{q(\mathbf{v}_{t|1}=\mathbf{v}_{t})}\biggl)\frac{q(\mathbf{v}_{t|1}=\mathbf{v}_{t})}{q(\mathbf{v}_{t|0}=\mathbf{v}_{t})}\mathcal{D}_{\text{KL}}(q(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|1}^{i}|\mathbf{v}_{t|1}=\mathbf{v}_{t})\|q(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|0}^{i}|\mathbf{v}_{t|0}=\mathbf{v}_{t}))\biggl]

(176)

Now consider
1-\frac{q(\mathbf{v}_{t|0}=\mathbf{v}_{t})}{q(\mathbf{v}_{t|1}=\mathbf{v}_{t})}
:

\displaystyle 1-\frac{q(\mathbf{v}_{t|0}=\mathbf{v}_{t})}{q(\mathbf{v}_{t|1}=\mathbf{v}_{t})}=\frac{1}{s+1}\cdot(1-\frac{s}{\zeta(\mathcal{V}_{1},\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{v}_{t},t)})\leq\frac{1}{s+1}\cdot\mathbbm{1}_{\mathbf{v}_{t}\in\Omega}

(177)

where
\Omega=\{\mathbf{v}_{t}|\zeta(\mathcal{V}_{1},\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{v}_{t},t)>s\}
. Therefore,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{v}_{t}\sim(q(\mathbf{v}_{t|1})-q(\mathbf{v}_{t|0}))}[\mathcal{D}_{\text{KL}}(q(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|1}^{i}|\mathbf{v}_{t|1}=\mathbf{v}_{t})\|q(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|0}^{i}|\mathbf{v}_{t|0}=\mathbf{v}_{t}))]

(178)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{v}_{t}\sim q(\mathbf{v}_{t|0}),\mathbf{v}_{t}\in\Omega}\biggl[\frac{1}{1+s}\sum_{\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i}=1}^{k}\frac{q(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|1}^{i}=\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i},\mathbf{v}_{t|1}=\mathbf{v}_{t})}{q(\mathbf{v}_{t|0}=\mathbf{v}_{t})}\log\frac{q(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|1}^{i}=\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i}|\mathbf{v}_{t|1}=\mathbf{v}_{t})}{q(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|0}^{i}=\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i}|\mathbf{v}_{t|0}=\mathbf{v}_{t})}\biggl]

(179)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{v}_{t}\sim q(\mathbf{v}_{t|0}),\mathbf{v}_{t}\in\Omega}\biggl[\frac{1}{s}\cdot\frac{\sum_{\mathbf{v}_{0}\in\mathcal{V}_{1}}\tau(\mathbf{v}_{0}^{i},\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i},\mathbf{v}_{t}^{i})\cdot(\bar{R}_{t})^{\bar{\omega}(\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{v}_{t})-\bar{\omega}(\mathbf{v}_{0},\mathbf{v}_{t})}}{1+\zeta(\mathcal{V}_{1},\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{v}_{t},t)}\log\frac{q(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|1}^{i}=\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i}|\mathbf{v}_{t|1}=\mathbf{v}_{t})}{q(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|0}^{i}=\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i}|\mathbf{v}_{t|0}=\mathbf{v}_{t})}\biggl]

(180)

Now consider
\log\frac{q(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|1}^{i}=\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i}|\mathbf{v}_{t|1}=\mathbf{v}_{t})}{q(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|0}^{i}=\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i}|\mathbf{v}_{t|0}=\mathbf{v}_{t})}
:

\displaystyle\log\frac{q(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|1}^{i}=\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i}|\mathbf{v}_{t|1}=\mathbf{v}_{t})}{q(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|0}^{i}=\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i}|\mathbf{v}_{t|0}=\mathbf{v}_{t})}=\log\frac{q(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|1}^{i}=\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i},\mathbf{v}_{t|1}=\mathbf{v}_{t})q(\mathbf{v}_{t|0}=\mathbf{v}_{t})}{q(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|0}^{i}=\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i},\mathbf{v}_{t|0}=\mathbf{v}_{t})q(\mathbf{v}_{t|1}=\mathbf{v}_{t})}

(181)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\log(1-\frac{1}{1+\sum_{\mathbf{v}_{0}\in\mathcal{V}_{1}}\frac{\tau(\mathbf{v}_{0}^{i},\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i},\mathbf{v}_{t}^{i})}{\tau(\mathbf{v}^{*i},\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i},\mathbf{v}_{t}^{i})}(\bar{R}_{t})^{\bar{\omega}(\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{v}_{t})-\bar{\omega}(\mathbf{v}_{0},\mathbf{v}_{t})}})(1+\frac{1}{\zeta(\mathcal{V}_{1},\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{v}_{t},t)})

(182)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\log\biggl[(1+\frac{\sum_{\mathbf{v}_{0}\in\mathcal{V}_{1}}(\frac{\tau(\mathbf{v}_{0}^{i},\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i},\mathbf{v}_{t}^{i})}{\tau(\mathbf{v}^{*i},\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i},\mathbf{v}_{t}^{i})}-1)(\bar{R}_{t})^{\bar{\omega}(\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{v}_{t})-\bar{\omega}(\mathbf{v}_{0},\mathbf{v}_{t})}}{(1+\sum_{\mathbf{v}_{0}\in\mathcal{V}_{1}}\frac{\tau(\mathbf{v}_{0}^{i},\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i},\mathbf{v}_{t}^{i})}{\tau(\mathbf{v}^{*i},\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i},\mathbf{v}_{t}^{i})}(\bar{R}_{t})^{\bar{\omega}(\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{v}_{t})-\bar{\omega}(\mathbf{v}_{0},\mathbf{v}_{t})})\cdot\zeta(\mathcal{V}_{1},\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{v}_{t},t)})\biggl]

(183)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{\max_{\mathbf{v}_{0}^{i}}\frac{\tau(\mathbf{v}_{0}^{i},\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i},\mathbf{v}_{t}^{i})}{\tau(\mathbf{v}^{*i},\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i},\mathbf{v}_{t}^{i})}-1}{\max_{\mathbf{v}_{0}^{i}}\frac{\tau(\mathbf{v}_{0}^{i},\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i},\mathbf{v}_{t}^{i})}{\tau(\mathbf{v}^{*i},\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i},\mathbf{v}_{t}^{i})}\cdot\zeta(\mathcal{V}_{1},\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{v}_{t},t)+1}

(184)

1. When
\mathbf{v}_{t}^{i}=\mathbf{v}^{*i}
, we further compare
\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i},\mathbf{v}_{t}^{i}
: (1) If
\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i}=\mathbf{v}_{t}^{i}
, we have
\frac{\tau(\mathbf{v}_{0}^{i},\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i},\mathbf{v}_{t}^{i})}{\tau(\mathbf{v}^{*i},\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i},\mathbf{v}_{t}^{i})}\leq 1
. (2) If
\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i}\neq\mathbf{v}_{t}^{i}
, we get
\frac{\tau(\mathbf{v}_{0}^{i},\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i},\mathbf{v}_{t}^{i})}{\tau(\mathbf{v}^{*i},\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i},\mathbf{v}_{t}^{i})}\leq\bar{R}_{t}\bar{R}_{t-1}
. Hence,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{v}_{t}\sim(q(\mathbf{v}_{t|1})-q(\mathbf{v}_{t|0}))}[\mathcal{D}_{\text{KL}}(q(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|1}^{i}|\mathbf{v}_{t|1}=\mathbf{v}_{t})\|q(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|0}^{i}|\mathbf{v}_{t|0}=\mathbf{v}_{t}))]

(185)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{v}_{t}\sim q(\mathbf{v}_{t|0}),\mathbf{v}_{t}\in\Omega}\biggl[\frac{1}{s}\frac{\sum_{\mathbf{v}_{0}\in\mathcal{V}_{1}}\tau(\mathbf{v}_{0}^{i},\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i},\mathbf{v}_{t}^{i})\cdot(\bar{R}_{t})^{\bar{\omega}(\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{v}_{t})-\bar{\omega}(\mathbf{v}_{0},\mathbf{v}_{t})}}{1+\zeta(\mathcal{V}_{1},\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{v}_{t},t)}\log\frac{q(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|1}^{i}=\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i}|\mathbf{v}_{t|1}=\mathbf{v}_{t})}{q(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|0}^{i}=\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i}|\mathbf{v}_{t|0}=\mathbf{v}_{t})}\biggl]

(186)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{v}_{t}\sim q(\mathbf{v}_{t|0}),\mathbf{v}_{t}\in\Omega}\biggl[\frac{1}{s}\frac{\sum_{\mathbf{v}_{0}\in\mathcal{V}_{1}}\sum_{\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i}\neq\mathbf{v}_{t}^{i}}\tau(\mathbf{v}_{0}^{i},\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i},\mathbf{v}_{t}^{i})\cdot(\bar{R}_{t})^{\bar{\omega}(\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{v}_{t})-\bar{\omega}(\mathbf{v}_{0},\mathbf{v}_{t})}}{1+\zeta(\mathcal{V}_{1},\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{v}_{t},t)}\cdot\frac{\bar{R}_{t}\bar{R}_{t-1}-1}{\bar{R}_{t}\bar{R}_{t-1}\zeta(\mathcal{V}_{1},\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{v}_{t},t)}\biggl]

(187)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{v}_{t}\sim q(\mathbf{v}_{t|0}),\mathbf{v}_{t}\in\Omega}\biggl[\frac{1}{s}\frac{\sum_{\mathbf{v}_{0}\in\mathcal{V}_{1}}(1-\tau(\mathbf{v}_{0}^{i},\mathbf{v}_{t}^{i},\mathbf{v}_{t}^{i}))\cdot(\bar{R}_{t})^{\bar{\omega}(\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{v}_{t})-\bar{\omega}(\mathbf{v}_{0},\mathbf{v}_{t})}}{1+\zeta(\mathcal{V}_{1},\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{v}_{t},t)}\cdot\frac{\bar{R}_{t}\bar{R}_{t-1}-1}{\bar{R}_{t}\bar{R}_{t-1}\zeta(\mathcal{V}_{1},\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{v}_{t},t)}\biggl]

(188)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{v}_{t}\sim q(\mathbf{v}_{t|0}),\mathbf{v}_{t}\in\Omega}\biggl[\frac{(1-\frac{\mu_{t}^{+}\bar{\mu}_{t-1}^{+}}{\bar{\mu}_{t}^{+}})\cdot(\bar{R}_{t}\bar{R}_{t-1}-1)}{\bar{R}_{t}\bar{R}_{t-1}\cdot s(1+s)}\biggl]

(189)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{s(s+1)}\cdot(1-\frac{\mu_{t}^{+}\bar{\mu}_{t-1}^{+}}{\bar{\mu}_{t}^{+}})\cdot(1-\frac{1}{\bar{R}_{t}\bar{R}_{t-1}})\cdot\mathbb{P}(\mathbf{v}_{t|0}\in\Omega)

(190)

2. When
\mathbf{v}_{t}^{i}\neq\mathbf{v}^{*i}
, similarly, (1) If
\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i}=\mathbf{v}_{t}^{i}
, we have
\frac{\tau(\mathbf{v}_{0}^{i},\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i},\mathbf{v}_{t}^{i})}{\tau(\mathbf{v}^{*i},\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i},\mathbf{v}_{t}^{i})}\leq\bar{R}_{t-1}/\bar{R}_{t}
. (2) If
\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i}\neq\mathbf{v}_{t}^{i}
, we have
\frac{\tau(\mathbf{v}_{0}^{i},\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i},\mathbf{v}_{t}^{i})}{\tau(\mathbf{v}^{*i},\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i},\mathbf{v}_{t}^{i})}\leq\bar{R}_{t-1}
. Therefore

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{v}_{t}\sim(q(\mathbf{v}_{t|1})-q(\mathbf{v}_{t|0}))}[\mathcal{D}_{\text{KL}}(q(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|1}^{i}|\mathbf{v}_{t|1}=\mathbf{v}_{t})\|q(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|0}^{i}|\mathbf{v}_{t|0}=\mathbf{v}_{t}))]

(191)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{v}_{t}\sim q(\mathbf{v}_{t|0}),\mathbf{v}_{t}\in\Omega}\biggl[\frac{1}{s}\frac{\sum_{\mathbf{v}_{0}\in\mathcal{V}_{1}}\tau(\mathbf{v}_{0}^{i},\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i},\mathbf{v}_{t}^{i})\cdot(\bar{R}_{t})^{\bar{\omega}(\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{v}_{t})-\bar{\omega}(\mathbf{v}_{0},\mathbf{v}_{t})}}{1+\zeta(\mathcal{V}_{1},\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{v}_{t},t)}\log\frac{q(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|1}^{i}=\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i}|\mathbf{v}_{t|1}=\mathbf{v}_{t})}{q(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|0}^{i}=\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i}|\mathbf{v}_{t|0}=\mathbf{v}_{t})}\biggl]

(192)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{v}_{t}\sim q(\mathbf{v}_{t|0}),\mathbf{v}_{t}\in\Omega}\biggl[\frac{1}{s}\frac{\sum_{\mathbf{v}_{0}\in\mathcal{V}_{1}}\sum_{\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i}=\mathbf{v}_{t}^{i}}\tau(\mathbf{v}_{0}^{i},\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i},\mathbf{v}_{t}^{i})\cdot(\bar{R}_{t})^{\bar{\omega}(\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{v}_{t})-\bar{\omega}(\mathbf{v}_{0},\mathbf{v}_{t})}}{1+\zeta(\mathcal{V}_{1},\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{v}_{t},t)}\frac{\bar{R}_{t-1}/\bar{R}_{t}-1}{\bar{R}_{t-1}/\bar{R}_{t}\zeta(\mathcal{V}_{1},\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{v}_{t},t)}

(193)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{1}{s}\cdot\frac{\sum_{\mathbf{v}_{0}\in\mathcal{V}_{1}}\sum_{\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i}\neq\mathbf{v}_{t}^{i}}\tau(\mathbf{v}_{0}^{i},\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i},\mathbf{v}_{t}^{i})\cdot(\bar{R}_{t})^{\bar{\omega}(\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{v}_{t})-\bar{\omega}(\mathbf{v}_{0},\mathbf{v}_{t})}}{1+\zeta(\mathcal{V}_{1},\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{v}_{t},t)}\frac{\bar{R}_{t-1}-1}{\bar{R}_{t-1}\zeta(\mathcal{V}_{1},\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{v}_{t},t)}\biggl]

(194)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{v}_{t}\sim q(\mathbf{v}_{t|0}),\mathbf{v}_{t}\in\Omega}\biggl[\frac{1}{s}\frac{\sum_{\mathbf{v}_{0}\in\mathcal{V}_{1}}(\bar{R}_{t-1}-1-(\bar{R}_{t}-1)\tau(\mathbf{v}_{0}^{i},\mathbf{v}_{t}^{i},\mathbf{v}_{t}^{i}))\cdot(\bar{R}_{t})^{\bar{\omega}(\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{v}_{t})-\bar{\omega}(\mathbf{v}_{0},\mathbf{v}_{t})}}{(1+\zeta(\mathcal{V}_{1},\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{v}_{t},t))\bar{R}_{t-1}\zeta(\mathcal{V}_{1},\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{v}_{t},t)}\biggl]

(195)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{v}_{t}\sim q(\mathbf{v}_{t|0}),\mathbf{v}_{t}\in\Omega}\biggl[\frac{((\bar{R}_{t-1}-1)-\frac{\mu_{t}^{+}\bar{\mu}_{t-1}^{+}}{\bar{\mu}_{t}^{+}}(\bar{R}_{t}-1))}{\bar{R}_{t-1}s(1+s)}\biggl]

(196)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{s(s+1)}\cdot\biggl[(1-\frac{\mu_{t}^{+}\bar{\mu}_{t-1}^{+}}{\bar{\mu}_{t}^{+}})\cdot(1-\frac{1}{\bar{R}_{t-1}})+\frac{\mu_{t}^{+}\bar{\mu}_{t-1}^{+}}{\bar{\mu}_{t}^{+}}\cdot(1-\frac{\bar{R}_{t}}{\bar{R}_{t-1}})\biggl]\cdot\mathbb{P}(\mathbf{v}_{t|0}\in\Omega)

(197)

Compare two bounds Eq. (
190
) and Eq. (
197
), since we have

\displaystyle(1-\frac{\mu_{t}^{+}\bar{\mu}_{t-1}^{+}}{\bar{\mu}_{t}^{+}})(1-\frac{1}{\bar{R}_{t-1}})+\frac{\mu_{t}^{+}\bar{\mu}_{t-1}^{+}}{\bar{\mu}_{t}^{+}}(1-\frac{\bar{R}_{t}}{\bar{R}_{t-1}})>(1-\frac{\mu_{t}^{+}\bar{\mu}_{t-1}^{+}}{\bar{\mu}_{t}^{+}})(1-\frac{1}{\bar{R}_{t}\bar{R}_{t-1}})

(198)

We derive the following bound:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{v}_{t}\sim(q(\mathbf{v}_{t|1})-q(\mathbf{v}_{t|0}))}[\mathcal{D}_{\text{KL}}(q(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|1}^{i}|\mathbf{v}_{t|1}=\mathbf{v}_{t})\|q(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|0}^{i}|\mathbf{v}_{t|0}=\mathbf{v}_{t}))]

(199)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{s(s+1)}\cdot\biggl[(1-\frac{\mu_{t}^{+}\bar{\mu}_{t-1}^{+}}{\bar{\mu}_{t}^{+}})\cdot(1-\frac{1}{\bar{R}_{t-1}})+\frac{\mu_{t}^{+}\bar{\mu}_{t-1}^{+}}{\bar{\mu}_{t}^{+}}\cdot(1-\frac{\bar{R}_{t}}{\bar{R}_{t-1}})\biggl]\cdot\mathbb{P}(\mathbf{v}_{t|0}\in\Omega)

(200)

∎

J.7
Proof of Lemma
D.1

Proof.

Given adjacent datasets
\mathcal{V}_{0},\mathcal{V}_{1}
and diffusion coefficients
\{\alpha_{t}\}_{t\in[T]}
, we consider the probability transitions in the generation process.

\displaystyle p_{\phi}(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|j}|\mathbf{v}_{t|j})=

\displaystyle p_{\phi}(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|j}^{1}|\mathbf{v}_{t|j})\cdot p_{\phi}(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|j}^{2}|\mathbf{v}_{t|j})

(201)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\prod_{i=1}^{2}[\sum_{\mathbf{v}_{0|j}^{i}=0}^{1}q(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|j}^{i},\mathbf{v}_{0|j}^{i}|\mathbf{v}_{t|j}^{i})p_{\phi}(\mathbf{v}_{0|j}^{i}|\mathbf{v}_{t|j})]

(202)

where
j\in\{0,1\}
denote the dataset index.

For
q(\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i},\mathbf{v}_{0}^{i}|\mathbf{v}_{t}^{i})
, from Lemma
K.2
, we obtain

\displaystyle q(\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i}|\mathbf{v}_{t}^{i},\mathbf{v}_{0}^{i})=\left\{\begin{aligned} &\text{When }\mathbf{v}_{0}^{i}=\mathbf{v}_{t}^{i}\left\{\begin{aligned} \text{If }\mathbf{v}_{0}^{i}=\mathbf{v}_{t}^{i}=\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i},\frac{\mu_{t}^{+}\cdot\bar{\mu}_{t-1}^{+}}{\bar{\mu}_{t}^{+}}=:C_{1,t}\\
\text{If }\mathbf{v}_{0}^{i}=\mathbf{v}_{t}^{i}\neq\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i},\frac{\mu_{t}^{-}\cdot\bar{\mu}_{t-1}^{-}}{\bar{\mu}_{t}^{+}}=:C_{2,t}\\
\end{aligned}\right.\\
&\text{When }\mathbf{v}_{0}^{i}\neq\mathbf{v}_{t}^{i}\left\{\begin{aligned} \text{If }\mathbf{v}_{0}^{i}\neq\mathbf{v}_{t}^{i}=\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i},\frac{\mu_{t}^{+}\cdot\bar{\mu}_{t-1}^{-}}{\bar{\mu}_{t}^{-}}=:\tilde{C}_{1,t}\\
\text{If }\mathbf{v}_{t}^{i}\neq\mathbf{v}_{0}^{i}=\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i},\frac{\mu_{t}^{-}\cdot\bar{\mu}_{t-1}^{+}}{\bar{\mu}_{t}^{-}}=:\tilde{C}_{2,t}\end{aligned}\right.\end{aligned}\right.

(203)

where we have
C_{1,t}+C_{2,t}=\tilde{C}_{1,t}+\tilde{C}_{2,t}
and
C_{1,t}>\tilde{C}_{1,t},C_{2,t}<\tilde{C}_{2,t}
.

Now, consider
q(\mathbf{v}_{t|1})
and
q(\mathbf{v}_{t|0})
. From the forward diffusion process, we have

\displaystyle q(\mathbf{v}_{t|1}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
1\end{bmatrix})=\frac{(\bar{\mu}_{t}^{+})^{2}+(s-1)\cdot(\bar{\mu}_{t}^{-})^{2}}{s},q(\mathbf{v}_{t|1}=\begin{bmatrix}0\\
0\end{bmatrix})=\frac{(\bar{\mu}_{t}^{-})^{2}+(s-1)\cdot(\bar{\mu}_{t}^{+})^{2}}{s},

(204)

\displaystyle q(\mathbf{v}_{t|1}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
0\end{bmatrix})=q(\mathbf{v}_{t|1}=\begin{bmatrix}0\\
1\end{bmatrix})=\bar{\mu}_{t}^{+}\cdot\bar{\mu}_{t}^{-}.

(205)

and

\displaystyle q(\mathbf{v}_{t|0}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
1\end{bmatrix})=\frac{2\cdot(\bar{\mu}_{t}^{+})^{2}+(s-1)\cdot(\bar{\mu}_{t}^{-})^{2}}{s+1},q(\mathbf{v}_{t|0}=\begin{bmatrix}0\\
0\end{bmatrix})=\frac{2\cdot(\bar{\mu}_{t}^{-})^{2}+(s-1)\cdot(\bar{\mu}_{t}^{+})^{2}}{s+1},

(206)

\displaystyle q(\mathbf{v}_{t|0}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
0\end{bmatrix})=q(\mathbf{v}_{t|1}=\begin{bmatrix}0\\
1\end{bmatrix})=\bar{\mu}_{t}^{+}\cdot\bar{\mu}_{t}^{-}.

(207)

Further, we consider the prediction probability
p_{\phi}(\mathbf{v}_{0|j}^{i}|\mathbf{v}_{t|j})
, which is determined by the training of denoising networks. In the training procedure, we are optimizing the following objective function

\displaystyle\text{minimize }\mathcal{D}_{\text{KL}}(q(\mathbf{v}_{0|j})\|\prod_{i=1}^{2}\sum_{\mathbf{v}_{t|1}}p_{\phi}(\mathbf{v}_{0|1}^{i}|\mathbf{v}_{t|1})q(\mathbf{v}_{t|1}))

(208)

From Lemma
K.8
, we obtain the optimal solution is when
p_{\phi}(\mathbf{v}_{0|1}^{i}|\mathbf{v}_{t|1})=q(\mathbf{v}_{0|1}^{i}|\mathbf{v}_{t|1})
.

Therefore,
p_{\phi}(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|j}|\mathbf{v}_{t|j})=\prod_{i=1}^{2}[\sum_{\mathbf{v}_{0|j}^{i}=0}^{1}q(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|j}^{i},\mathbf{v}_{0|j}^{i}|\mathbf{v}_{t|j}^{i})q(\mathbf{v}_{0|j}^{i}|\mathbf{v}_{t|j})]
. For
q(\mathbf{v}_{0|j}^{i}|\mathbf{v}_{t|j})]
, we have the following calculation: for dataset
\mathcal{V}_{1}
,

\displaystyle q(\mathbf{v}_{0|1}^{i}=0|\mathbf{v}_{t|1}=\begin{bmatrix}0\\
0\end{bmatrix})=\frac{(s-1)\cdot(\bar{\mu}_{t}^{+})^{2}}{(s-1)\cdot(\bar{\mu}_{t}^{+})^{2}+(\bar{\mu}_{t}^{-})^{2}},q(\mathbf{v}_{0|1}^{i}=1|\mathbf{v}_{t|1}=\begin{bmatrix}0\\
0\end{bmatrix})=\frac{(\bar{\mu}_{t}^{-})^{2}}{(s-1)\cdot(\bar{\mu}_{t}^{+})^{2}+(\bar{\mu}_{t}^{-})^{2}},

\displaystyle q(\mathbf{v}_{0|1}^{i}=0|\mathbf{v}_{t|1}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
1\end{bmatrix})=\frac{(s-1)\cdot(\bar{\mu}_{t}^{-})^{2}}{(s-1)\cdot(\bar{\mu}_{t}^{-})^{2}+(\bar{\mu}_{t}^{+})^{2}},q(\mathbf{v}_{0|1}^{i}=1|\mathbf{v}_{t|1}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
1\end{bmatrix})=\frac{(\bar{\mu}_{t}^{+})^{2}}{(s-1)\cdot(\bar{\mu}_{t}^{-})^{2}+(\bar{\mu}_{t}^{+})^{2}},

\displaystyle q(\mathbf{v}_{0|1}^{i}=0|\mathbf{v}_{t|1}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
0\end{bmatrix})=\frac{s-1}{s},q(\mathbf{v}_{0|1}^{i}=1|\mathbf{v}_{t|1}=\begin{bmatrix}0\\
1\end{bmatrix})=\frac{1}{s}.

and for dataset
\mathcal{V}_{0}

\displaystyle q(\mathbf{v}_{0|0}^{i}=0|\mathbf{v}_{t|0}=\begin{bmatrix}0\\
0\end{bmatrix})=\frac{(s-1)\cdot(\bar{\mu}_{t}^{+})^{2}}{(s-1)\cdot(\bar{\mu}_{t}^{+})^{2}+2\cdot(\bar{\mu}_{t}^{-})^{2}},q(\mathbf{v}_{0|0}^{i}=1|\mathbf{v}_{t|0}=\begin{bmatrix}0\\
0\end{bmatrix})=\frac{2\cdot(\bar{\mu}_{t}^{-})^{2}}{(s-1)\cdot(\bar{\mu}_{t}^{+})^{2}+2\cdot(\bar{\mu}_{t}^{-})^{2}},

\displaystyle q(\mathbf{v}_{0|0}^{i}=0|\mathbf{v}_{t|0}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
1\end{bmatrix})=\frac{(s-1)\cdot(\bar{\mu}_{t}^{-})^{2}}{(s-1)\cdot(\bar{\mu}_{t}^{-})^{2}+2\cdot(\bar{\mu}_{t}^{+})^{2}},q(\mathbf{v}_{0|0}^{i}=1|\mathbf{v}_{t|0}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
1\end{bmatrix})=\frac{2\cdot(\bar{\mu}_{t}^{+})^{2}}{(s-1)\cdot(\bar{\mu}_{t}^{-})^{2}+2\cdot(\bar{\mu}_{t}^{+})^{2}},

\displaystyle q(\mathbf{v}_{0|0}^{i}=0|\mathbf{v}_{t|0}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
0\end{bmatrix})=\frac{s-1}{s+1},q(\mathbf{v}_{0|0}^{i}=1|\mathbf{v}_{t|0}=\begin{bmatrix}0\\
1\end{bmatrix})=\frac{2}{s+1}.

For the convenience of further derivation, we introduce the notation
q_{j}(x_{1}|x_{2},x_{3}):=q(\mathbf{v}_{0|j}^{i}=x_{1}|\mathbf{v}_{t|j}=\begin{bmatrix}x_{1}\\
x_{2}\end{bmatrix})]
for simplicity. Therefore,

\displaystyle p_{\phi}(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|j}=\begin{bmatrix}0\\
0\end{bmatrix}|\mathbf{v}_{t|j}=\begin{bmatrix}0\\
0\end{bmatrix})=(C_{1,t}\cdot q_{j}(0|0,0)+\tilde{C}_{1,t}\cdot q_{j}(1|0,0))^{2}

\displaystyle p_{\phi}(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|j}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
1\end{bmatrix}|\mathbf{v}_{t|j}=\begin{bmatrix}0\\
0\end{bmatrix})=(C_{2,t}\cdot q_{j}(0|0,0)+\tilde{C}_{2,t}\cdot q_{j}(1|0,0))^{2}

\displaystyle p_{\phi}(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|j}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
0\end{bmatrix}|\mathbf{v}_{t|j}=\begin{bmatrix}0\\
0\end{bmatrix})=(C_{2,t}\cdot q_{j}(0|0,0)+\tilde{C}_{2,t}\cdot q_{j}(1|0,0))(C_{1,t}\cdot q_{j}(0|0,0)+\tilde{C}_{1,t}\cdot q_{j}(1|0,0))

\displaystyle p_{\phi}(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|j}=\begin{bmatrix}0\\
1\end{bmatrix}|\mathbf{v}_{t|j}=\begin{bmatrix}0\\
0\end{bmatrix})=(C_{2,t}\cdot q_{j}(0|0,0)+\tilde{C}_{2,t}\cdot q_{j}(1|0,0))(C_{1,t}\cdot q_{j}(0|0,0)+\tilde{C}_{1,t}\cdot q_{j}(1|0,0))

\displaystyle p_{\phi}(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|j}=\begin{bmatrix}0\\
0\end{bmatrix}|\mathbf{v}_{t|j}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
1\end{bmatrix})=(\tilde{C}_{2,t}\cdot q_{j}(0|1,1)+C_{2,t}\cdot q_{j}(1|1,1))^{2}

\displaystyle p_{\phi}(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|j}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
1\end{bmatrix}|\mathbf{v}_{t|j}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
1\end{bmatrix})=(\tilde{C}_{1,t}\cdot q_{j}(0|1,1)+C_{1,t}\cdot q_{j}(1|1,1))^{2}

\displaystyle p_{\phi}(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|j}=\begin{bmatrix}0\\
1\end{bmatrix}|\mathbf{v}_{t|j}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
1\end{bmatrix})=(\tilde{C}_{1,t}\cdot q_{j}(0|1,1)+C_{1,t}\cdot q_{j}(1|1,1))(\tilde{C}_{2,t}\cdot q_{j}(0|1,1)+C_{2,t}\cdot q_{j}(1|1,1))

\displaystyle p_{\phi}(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|j}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
0\end{bmatrix}|\mathbf{v}_{t|j}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
1\end{bmatrix})=(\tilde{C}_{1,t}\cdot q_{j}(0|1,1)+C_{1,t}\cdot q_{j}(1|1,1))(\tilde{C}_{2,t}\cdot q_{j}(0|1,1)+C_{2,t}\cdot q_{j}(1|1,1))

\displaystyle p_{\phi}(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|j}=\begin{bmatrix}0\\
0\end{bmatrix}|\mathbf{v}_{t|j}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
0\end{bmatrix})=(\tilde{C}_{1,t}\cdot q_{j}(1|1,0)+C_{1,t}\cdot q_{j}(0|1,0))(\tilde{C}_{2,t}\cdot q_{j}(0|1,0)+C_{2,t}\cdot q_{j}(1|1,0))

\displaystyle p_{\phi}(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|j}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
1\end{bmatrix}|\mathbf{v}_{t|j}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
0\end{bmatrix})=(\tilde{C}_{1,t}\cdot q_{j}(0|1,0)+C_{1,t}\cdot q_{j}(1|1,0))(\tilde{C}_{2,t}\cdot q_{j}(1|1,0)+C_{2,t}\cdot q_{j}(0|1,0))

\displaystyle p_{\phi}(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|j}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
0\end{bmatrix}|\mathbf{v}_{t|j}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
0\end{bmatrix})=(\tilde{C}_{1,t}\cdot q_{j}(0|1,0)+C_{1,t}\cdot q_{j}(1|1,0))(\tilde{C}_{1,t}\cdot q_{j}(1|1,0)+C_{1,t}\cdot q_{j}(0|1,0))

\displaystyle p_{\phi}(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|j}=\begin{bmatrix}0\\
1\end{bmatrix}|\mathbf{v}_{t|j}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
0\end{bmatrix})=(\tilde{C}_{2,t}\cdot q_{j}(0|1,0)+C_{2,t}\cdot q_{j}(1|1,0))(\tilde{C}_{2,t}\cdot q_{j}(1|1,0)+C_{2,t}\cdot q_{j}(0|1,0))

\displaystyle p_{\phi}(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|j}=\begin{bmatrix}0\\
0\end{bmatrix}|\mathbf{v}_{t|j}=\begin{bmatrix}0\\
1\end{bmatrix})=(\tilde{C}_{1,t}\cdot q_{j}(1|1,0)+C_{1,t}\cdot q_{j}(0|1,0))(\tilde{C}_{2,t}\cdot q_{j}(0|1,0)+C_{2,t}\cdot q_{j}(1|1,0))

\displaystyle p_{\phi}(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|j}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
1\end{bmatrix}|\mathbf{v}_{t|j}=\begin{bmatrix}0\\
1\end{bmatrix})=(\tilde{C}_{1,t}\cdot q_{j}(0|1,0)+C_{1,t}\cdot q_{j}(1|1,0))(\tilde{C}_{2,t}\cdot q_{j}(1|1,0)+C_{2,t}\cdot q_{j}(0|1,0))

\displaystyle p_{\phi}(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|j}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
0\end{bmatrix}|\mathbf{v}_{t|j}=\begin{bmatrix}0\\
1\end{bmatrix})=(\tilde{C}_{2,t}\cdot q_{j}(0|1,0)+C_{2,t}\cdot q_{j}(1|1,0))(\tilde{C}_{2,t}\cdot q_{j}(1|1,0)+C_{2,t}\cdot q_{j}(0|1,0))

\displaystyle p_{\phi}(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|j}=\begin{bmatrix}0\\
1\end{bmatrix}|\mathbf{v}_{t|j}=\begin{bmatrix}0\\
1\end{bmatrix})=(\tilde{C}_{1,t}\cdot q_{j}(0|1,0)+C_{1,t}\cdot q_{j}(1|1,0))(\tilde{C}_{1,t}\cdot q_{j}(1|1,0)+C_{1,t}\cdot q_{j}(0|1,0))

With above the circumstances discussed above, we define
H_{t-1}=p_{\phi}(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|0}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
1\end{bmatrix})-p_{\phi}(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|1}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
1\end{bmatrix})
, we have

\displaystyle H_{t-1}=

\displaystyle p_{\phi}(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|0}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
1\end{bmatrix}|\mathbf{v}_{t|0}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
1\end{bmatrix})p_{\phi}(\mathbf{v}_{t|0}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
1\end{bmatrix})+p_{\phi}(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|0}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
1\end{bmatrix}|\mathbf{v}_{t|0}=\begin{bmatrix}0\\
0\end{bmatrix})p_{\phi}(\mathbf{v}_{t|0}=\begin{bmatrix}0\\
0\end{bmatrix})

\displaystyle+p_{\phi}(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|0}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
1\end{bmatrix}|\mathbf{v}_{t|0}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
0\end{bmatrix})p_{\phi}(\mathbf{v}_{t|0}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
0\end{bmatrix})+p_{\phi}(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|0}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
1\end{bmatrix}|\mathbf{v}_{t|0}=\begin{bmatrix}0\\
1\end{bmatrix})p_{\phi}(\mathbf{v}_{t|0}=\begin{bmatrix}0\\
1\end{bmatrix})

\displaystyle-p_{\phi}(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|1}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
1\end{bmatrix}|\mathbf{v}_{t|1}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
1\end{bmatrix})p_{\phi}(\mathbf{v}_{t|1}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
1\end{bmatrix})+p_{\phi}(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|1}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
1\end{bmatrix}|\mathbf{v}_{t|1}=\begin{bmatrix}0\\
0\end{bmatrix})p_{\phi}(\mathbf{v}_{t|1}=\begin{bmatrix}0\\
0\end{bmatrix})

\displaystyle-p_{\phi}(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|1}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
1\end{bmatrix}|\mathbf{v}_{t|1}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
0\end{bmatrix})p_{\phi}(\mathbf{v}_{t|1}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
0\end{bmatrix})+p_{\phi}(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|1}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
1\end{bmatrix}|\mathbf{v}_{t|1}=\begin{bmatrix}0\\
1\end{bmatrix})p_{\phi}(\mathbf{v}_{t|1}=\begin{bmatrix}0\\
1\end{bmatrix})

(209)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle p_{\phi}(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|0}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
1\end{bmatrix}|\mathbf{v}_{t|0}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
1\end{bmatrix})(p_{\phi}(\mathbf{v}_{t|0}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
1\end{bmatrix})-p_{\phi}(\mathbf{v}_{t|1}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
1\end{bmatrix}))

\displaystyle+p_{\phi}(\mathbf{v}_{t|1}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
1\end{bmatrix})(p_{\phi}(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|0}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
1\end{bmatrix}|\mathbf{v}_{t|0}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
1\end{bmatrix})-p_{\phi}(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|1}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
1\end{bmatrix}|\mathbf{v}_{t|1}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
1\end{bmatrix}))

\displaystyle+p_{\phi}(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|0}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
1\end{bmatrix}|\mathbf{v}_{t|0}=\begin{bmatrix}0\\
0\end{bmatrix})(p_{\phi}(\mathbf{v}_{t|0}=\begin{bmatrix}0\\
0\end{bmatrix})-p_{\phi}(\mathbf{v}_{t|1}=\begin{bmatrix}0\\
0\end{bmatrix}))

\displaystyle+p_{\phi}(\mathbf{v}_{t|1}=\begin{bmatrix}0\\
0\end{bmatrix})(p_{\phi}(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|0}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
1\end{bmatrix}|\mathbf{v}_{t|0}=\begin{bmatrix}0\\
0\end{bmatrix})-p_{\phi}(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|1}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
1\end{bmatrix}|\mathbf{v}_{t|1}=\begin{bmatrix}0\\
0\end{bmatrix}))

\displaystyle+p_{\phi}(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|0}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
1\end{bmatrix}|\mathbf{v}_{t|0}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
0\end{bmatrix})(p_{\phi}(\mathbf{v}_{t|0}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
0\end{bmatrix})-p_{\phi}(\mathbf{v}_{t|1}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
0\end{bmatrix}))

\displaystyle+p_{\phi}(\mathbf{v}_{t|1}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
0\end{bmatrix})(p_{\phi}(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|0}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
1\end{bmatrix}|\mathbf{v}_{t|0}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
0\end{bmatrix})-p_{\phi}(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|1}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
1\end{bmatrix}|\mathbf{v}_{t|1}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
0\end{bmatrix}))

\displaystyle+p_{\phi}(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|0}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
1\end{bmatrix}|\mathbf{v}_{t|0}=\begin{bmatrix}0\\
1\end{bmatrix})(p_{\phi}(\mathbf{v}_{t|0}=\begin{bmatrix}0\\
1\end{bmatrix})-p_{\phi}(\mathbf{v}_{t|1}=\begin{bmatrix}0\\
1\end{bmatrix}))

\displaystyle+p_{\phi}(\mathbf{v}_{t|1}=\begin{bmatrix}0\\
1\end{bmatrix})(p_{\phi}(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|0}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
1\end{bmatrix}|\mathbf{v}_{t|0}=\begin{bmatrix}0\\
1\end{bmatrix})-p_{\phi}(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|1}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
1\end{bmatrix}|\mathbf{v}_{t|1}=\begin{bmatrix}0\\
1\end{bmatrix}))

Since
p_{\phi}(\mathbf{v}_{t|0}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
1\end{bmatrix})-p_{\phi}(\mathbf{v}_{t|1}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
1\end{bmatrix})=-\sum_{\mathbf{v}_{t}\neq[1,1]^{T}}(p_{\phi}(\mathbf{v}_{t|0}=\mathbf{v}_{t})-p_{\phi}(\mathbf{v}_{t|1}=\mathbf{v}_{t}))
and
\max\biggl\{p_{\phi}(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|0}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
1\end{bmatrix}|\mathbf{v}_{t|0}=\begin{bmatrix}0\\
0\end{bmatrix}),p_{\phi}(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|0}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
1\end{bmatrix}|\mathbf{v}_{t|0}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
0\end{bmatrix}),p_{\phi}(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|0}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
1\end{bmatrix}|\mathbf{v}_{t|0}=\begin{bmatrix}0\\
1\end{bmatrix})\biggl\}=p_{\phi}(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|0}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
1\end{bmatrix}|\mathbf{v}_{t|0}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
0\end{bmatrix})=p_{\phi}(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|0}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
1\end{bmatrix}|\mathbf{v}_{t|0}=\begin{bmatrix}0\\
1\end{bmatrix})
, we have

\displaystyle H_{t-1}\geq

\displaystyle\biggl[p_{\phi}(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|0}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
1\end{bmatrix}|\mathbf{v}_{t|0}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
1\end{bmatrix})-p_{\phi}(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|0}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
1\end{bmatrix}|\mathbf{v}_{t|0}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
0\end{bmatrix})\biggl]\cdot H_{t}

\displaystyle+\min_{\mathbf{v}_{t}}\biggl[p_{\phi}(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|0}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
1\end{bmatrix}|\mathbf{v}_{t|0}=\mathbf{v}_{t})-p_{\phi}(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|1}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
1\end{bmatrix}|\mathbf{v}_{t|1}=\mathbf{v}_{t})\biggl]

(211)

For
\min_{\mathbf{v}_{t}}\biggl[p_{\phi}(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|0}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
1\end{bmatrix}|\mathbf{v}_{t|0}=\mathbf{v}_{t})-p_{\phi}(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|1}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
1\end{bmatrix}|\mathbf{v}_{t|1}=\mathbf{v}_{t})\biggl]
, we have

\displaystyle p_{\phi}(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|0}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
1\end{bmatrix}|\mathbf{v}_{t|0}=\begin{bmatrix}0\\
0\end{bmatrix})-p_{\phi}(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|1}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
1\end{bmatrix}|\mathbf{v}_{t|1}=\begin{bmatrix}0\\
0\end{bmatrix})

(212)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{[(s-1)(\tilde{C}_{2,t}-C_{2,t})(\bar{\mu}_{t}^{+})^{2}(\bar{\mu}_{t}^{-})^{2}][2(s-1)^{2}C_{2,t}(\bar{\mu}_{t}^{+})^{4}+3(s-1)(C_{2,t}+\tilde{C}_{2,t})(\bar{\mu}_{t}^{+})^{2}(\bar{\mu}_{t}^{-})^{2}+4\tilde{C}_{2,t}(\bar{\mu}_{t}^{-})^{4}]}{[(s-1)(\bar{\mu}_{t}^{+})^{2}+2(\bar{\mu}_{t}^{-})]^{2}[(s-1)(\bar{\mu}_{t}^{+})^{2}+(\bar{\mu}_{t}^{-})^{2}]^{2}}

(213)

\displaystyle p_{\phi}(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|0}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
1\end{bmatrix}|\mathbf{v}_{t|0}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
1\end{bmatrix})-p_{\phi}(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|1}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
1\end{bmatrix}|\mathbf{v}_{t|1}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
1\end{bmatrix})

(214)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{[(s-1)(C_{1,t}-\tilde{C}_{1,t})(\bar{\mu}_{t}^{+})^{2}(\bar{\mu}_{t}^{-})^{2}][2(s-1)^{2}C_{1,t}(\bar{\mu}_{t}^{+})^{4}+3(s-1)(C_{1,t}+\tilde{C}_{1,t})(\bar{\mu}_{t}^{+})^{2}(\bar{\mu}_{t}^{-})^{2}+4\tilde{C}_{1,t}(\bar{\mu}_{t}^{+})^{4}]}{[(s-1)(\bar{\mu}_{t}^{-})^{2}+2(\bar{\mu}_{t}^{+})]^{2}[(s-1)(\bar{\mu}_{t}^{-})^{2}+(\bar{\mu}_{t}^{+})^{2}]^{2}}

(215)

\displaystyle p_{\phi}(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|0}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
1\end{bmatrix}|\mathbf{v}_{t|0}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
1\end{bmatrix})-p_{\phi}(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|0}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
1\end{bmatrix}|\mathbf{v}_{t|1}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
0\end{bmatrix})

(216)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{(s-1)\{(s^{2}-2s-1)[(C_{1,t}\cdot C_{2,t}-\tilde{C}_{1,t}\cdot C_{2,t})+(\tilde{C}_{1,t}\cdot\tilde{C}_{2,t}-\tilde{C}_{1,t}\cdot C_{2,t})]+(3s+1)(C_{1,t}\cdot\tilde{C_{2,t}}-\tilde{C}_{1,t}\cdot\tilde{C}_{2,t})\}}{s^{2}(s+1)^{2}}

(217)

From detailed calculation, we can show that

\displaystyle\min_{\mathbf{v}_{t}}\biggl[p_{\phi}(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|0}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
1\end{bmatrix}|\mathbf{v}_{t|0}=\mathbf{v}_{t})-p_{\phi}(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|1}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
1\end{bmatrix}|\mathbf{v}_{t|1}=\mathbf{v}_{t})\biggl]

(218)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{[(s-1)(\tilde{C}_{2,t}-C_{2,t})(\bar{\mu}_{t}^{+})^{2}(\bar{\mu}_{t}^{-})^{2}][2(s-1)^{2}C_{2,t}(\bar{\mu}_{t}^{+})^{4}+3(s-1)(C_{2,t}+\tilde{C}_{2,t})(\bar{\mu}_{t}^{+})^{2}(\bar{\mu}_{t}^{-})^{2}+4\tilde{C}_{2,t}(\bar{\mu}_{t}^{-})^{4}]}{[(s-1)(\bar{\mu}_{t}^{+})^{2}+2(\bar{\mu}_{t}^{-})]^{2}[(s-1)(\bar{\mu}_{t}^{+})^{2}+(\bar{\mu}_{t}^{-})^{2}]^{2}}

(219)

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle G_{t}

(220)

For
t=0
, we have

\displaystyle p_{\phi}(\mathbf{v}_{1|1}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
1\end{bmatrix})(p_{\phi}(\mathbf{v}_{0|0}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
1\end{bmatrix}|\mathbf{v}_{1|0}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
1\end{bmatrix})-p_{\phi}(\mathbf{v}_{0|1}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
1\end{bmatrix}|\mathbf{v}_{1|1}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
1\end{bmatrix}))

\displaystyle+p_{\phi}(\mathbf{v}_{1|1}=\begin{bmatrix}0\\
0\end{bmatrix})(p_{\phi}(\mathbf{v}_{0|0}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
1\end{bmatrix}|\mathbf{v}_{1|0}=\begin{bmatrix}0\\
0\end{bmatrix})-p_{\phi}(\mathbf{v}_{0|1}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
1\end{bmatrix}|\mathbf{v}_{1|1}=\begin{bmatrix}0\\
0\end{bmatrix}))

\displaystyle+p_{\phi}(\mathbf{v}_{1|1}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
0\end{bmatrix})(p_{\phi}(\mathbf{v}_{0|0}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
1\end{bmatrix}|\mathbf{v}_{1|0}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
0\end{bmatrix})-p_{\phi}(\mathbf{v}_{0|1}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
1\end{bmatrix}|\mathbf{v}_{1|1}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
0\end{bmatrix}))

\displaystyle+p_{\phi}(\mathbf{v}_{1|1}=\begin{bmatrix}0\\
1\end{bmatrix})(p_{\phi}(\mathbf{v}_{0|0}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
1\end{bmatrix}|\mathbf{v}_{1|0}=\begin{bmatrix}0\\
1\end{bmatrix})-p_{\phi}(\mathbf{v}_{0|1}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
1\end{bmatrix}|\mathbf{v}_{1|1}=\begin{bmatrix}0\\
1\end{bmatrix}))

(221)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle p_{\phi}(\mathbf{v}_{1|1}=\begin{bmatrix}0\\
0\end{bmatrix})\cdot\frac{1}{s\bar{R}_{1}^{2}}+p_{\phi}(\mathbf{v}_{1|1}=\begin{bmatrix}0\\
0\end{bmatrix})\cdot\frac{2}{s}+p_{\phi}(\mathbf{v}_{1|1}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
1\end{bmatrix})\cdot\frac{\bar{R}_{1}^{2}}{s}

(222)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{s\bar{R}_{1}^{2}}=:G_{0}

(223)

Let
F_{t}:=p_{\phi}(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|0}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
1\end{bmatrix}|\mathbf{v}_{t|0}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
1\end{bmatrix})-p_{\phi}(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|0}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
1\end{bmatrix}|\mathbf{v}_{t|0}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
0\end{bmatrix})
, we have

\displaystyle F_{t}=\frac{4(\bar{\mu}_{t}^{+})^{4}A+4(s-1)(\bar{\mu}_{t}^{+})^{2}(\bar{\mu}_{t}^{-})^{2}B+(s-1)^{2}(\bar{\mu}_{t}^{-})^{4}C}{(s+1)^{2}[2(\bar{\mu}_{t}^{+})^{2}+(s-1)(\bar{\mu}_{t}^{-})^{2}]^{2}}

(224)

where

\displaystyle A=(s+1)^{2}C_{1,t}^{2}-[(s-1)\tilde{C}_{1,t}+2C_{1,t}][(s-1)C_{1,t}+2\tilde{C}_{2,t}],

(225)

\displaystyle B=(s+1)^{2}\tilde{C}_{1,t}\cdot C_{1,t}-[(s-1)\tilde{C}_{1,t}+2C_{1,t}][(s-1)C_{1,t}+2\tilde{C}_{2,t}],

(226)

\displaystyle C=(s+1)^{2}\tilde{C}_{1,t}^{2}-[(s-1)\tilde{C}_{1,t}+2C_{1,t}][(s-1)C_{1,t}+2\tilde{C}_{2,t}]

(227)

Given that
H_{T}=0
, and by iteratively applying the inequality
H_{t-1}\geq G_{t}+F_{t}H_{t}
, we arrive at the desired conclusion.
∎

J.8
Proof of Lemma
D.2

Proof.

Since
q(\mathbf{v}_{T|1}=\begin{bmatrix}0\\
0\end{bmatrix})=q(\mathbf{v}_{T|1}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
1\end{bmatrix})=q(\mathbf{v}_{T|1}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
0\end{bmatrix})=q(\mathbf{v}_{T|1}=\begin{bmatrix}0\\
1\end{bmatrix})=\frac{1}{4}
. From induction and symmetric properties, we obtain that for any
t
, we have

\displaystyle q(\mathbf{v}_{t|1}=\begin{bmatrix}0\\
0\end{bmatrix})\geq q(\mathbf{v}_{t|1}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
0\end{bmatrix})=q(\mathbf{v}_{t|1}=\begin{bmatrix}0\\
1\end{bmatrix})\geq q(\mathbf{v}_{t|1}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
1\end{bmatrix})

(228)

On the other hand,

\displaystyle p_{\phi}(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|1}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
1\end{bmatrix}|\mathbf{v}_{t|1}=\begin{bmatrix}0\\
0\end{bmatrix})<

\displaystyle p_{\phi}(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|1}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
1\end{bmatrix}|\mathbf{v}_{t|1}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
0\end{bmatrix})=p_{\phi}(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|1}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
1\end{bmatrix}|\mathbf{v}_{t|1}=\begin{bmatrix}0\\
1\end{bmatrix})

\displaystyle<

\displaystyle p_{\phi}(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|1}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
1\end{bmatrix}|\mathbf{v}_{t|1}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
1\end{bmatrix})

(229)

Thus, using Chebyshev’s inequality,

\displaystyle p_{\phi}(\mathbf{v}_{0|1}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
1\end{bmatrix})=\sum_{\mathbf{v}_{1}}p_{\phi}(\mathbf{v}_{0|1}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
1\end{bmatrix}|\mathbf{v}_{1|1}=\mathbf{v}_{1})p_{\phi}(\mathbf{v}_{1|1}=\mathbf{v}_{1})

(230)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle p_{\phi}(\mathbf{v}_{0|1}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
1\end{bmatrix}|\mathbf{v}_{1|1}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
0\end{bmatrix})+p_{\phi}(\mathbf{v}_{0|1}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
1\end{bmatrix}|\mathbf{v}_{1|1}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
1\end{bmatrix})p_{\phi}(\mathbf{v}_{1|1}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
1\end{bmatrix})

(231)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{s}+\frac{\bar{R}_{1}^{2}}{\bar{R}_{1}^{2}+s}\cdot\underbrace{(\frac{1}{4}\min_{t}\{(\sum_{\mathbf{v}_{1}}p_{\phi}(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|1}=\begin{bmatrix}1\\
1\end{bmatrix}|\mathbf{v}_{t|1}=\mathbf{v}_{1}))\})}_{\Delta}

(232)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Theta_{s}(\frac{1}{s})

(233)

where from proof of Lemma
D.1
, we have

\displaystyle\Delta=\min_{t}\frac{1}{4}[\frac{C_{2,t}\cdot s\cdot\bar{R}_{t}^{2}}{s\cdot\bar{R}_{t}^{2}+1}+\frac{\tilde{C}_{2,t}}{s\bar{R}_{t}^{2}+1}+\frac{\tilde{C}_{1,t}\cdot s}{s+\bar{R}_{t}^{2}}+\frac{C_{1,t}\cdot\bar{R}_{t}^{2}}{s+\bar{R}_{t}^{2}}+2(\frac{\tilde{C}_{1,t}\cdot(s-1)}{s}+\frac{C_{1,t}\cdot}{s})(\frac{C_{2,t}\cdot(s-1)}{s}+\frac{\tilde{C}_{2,t}}{s})].

(234)

∎

Appendix K
Additional Lemmas and Proofs

Lemma K.1

(Monotonicity of KL Divergence)
.

Let
P_{X_{1},X_{2},\dots,X_{T}},Q_{X_{1},X_{2},\dots,X_{T}}
be probability measures over random variables
X_{1},X_{2},\dots,X_{T}
, where
P_{X_{1}},Q_{X_{1}}
are the marginal measures of
P_{X_{1},X_{2},\dots,X_{T}}
and
Q_{X_{1},X_{2},\dots,X_{T}}
, respectively. Then, by the monotonicity property of KL divergence, we have:

\displaystyle\mathcal{D}_{\text{KL}}(P_{X_{1}}\|Q_{X_{1}})\leq\mathcal{D}_{\text{KL}}(P_{X_{1},X_{2},X_{3},...,X_{T}}\|Q_{X_{1},X_{2},...,X_{T}})

(235)

Proof of Lemma
K.1
.

The proof refer to
(
Polyanskiy 2020
)
.
∎

Lemma K.2

(Boundedness of Posterior Distribution)
.

The posterior distribution in discrete diffusion process is given as
q(\mathbf{v}_{t-1}|\mathbf{v}_{t},\mathbf{v}_{0})=\text{Cat}(\mathbf{v}_{t-1};\mathbf{v}_{t}Q_{t}^{T}\odot\mathbf{v}_{0}\overline{Q}_{t-1}/\mathbf{v}_{0}\overline{Q}_{t}\mathbf{v}_{t}^{T})
such that

\displaystyle\max_{\mathbf{v}_{0},\mathbf{v}_{t-1},\mathbf{v}_{t}}q(\mathbf{v}_{t-1}|\mathbf{v}_{t},\mathbf{v}_{0})=\frac{\mu_{t}^{+}\cdot\bar{\mu}_{t-1}^{+}}{\bar{\mu}_{t}^{+}},\min_{\mathbf{v}_{0},\mathbf{v}_{t-1},\mathbf{v}_{t}}q(\mathbf{v}_{t-1}|\mathbf{v}_{t},\mathbf{v}_{0})=\frac{\mu_{t}^{-}\cdot\bar{\mu}_{t-1}^{-}}{\bar{\mu}_{t}^{+}}.

(236)

Proof of Lemma
K.2
.

Since
\overline{Q}_{t}=\overline{\alpha}_{t}I+(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})\frac{\mathbbm{1}\mathbbm{1}^{T}}{k}
, from definition, given
l,j\in\{1,2,...,k\}

\displaystyle q(\mathbf{v}_{t-1}|\mathbf{v}_{t}=l,\mathbf{v}_{0}=j)=\frac{[\alpha_{t}I+(1-\alpha_{t})\frac{\mathbbm{1}\mathbbm{1}^{T}}{k}]_{l,\cdot}\odot[\overline{\alpha}_{t-1}I+(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t-1})\frac{\mathbbm{1}\mathbbm{1}^{T}}{k}]_{j,\cdot}}{[\overline{\alpha}_{t}I+(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})\frac{\mathbbm{1}\mathbbm{1}^{T}}{k}]_{j,l}}

(237)

\displaystyle[\alpha_{t}I+(1-\alpha_{t})\frac{\mathbbm{1}\mathbbm{1}^{T}}{k}]_{l,\cdot}=[\mu_{t}^{-},\mu_{t}^{-},...,\mu_{t}^{-},\mu_{t}^{+},\mu_{t}^{-},...,\mu_{t}^{-}]

(238)

\displaystyle[\overline{\alpha}_{t-1}I+(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t-1})\frac{\mathbbm{1}\mathbbm{1}^{T}}{k}]_{l,\cdot}=[\bar{\mu}_{t-1}^{-},\bar{\mu}_{t-1}^{-},...,\bar{\mu}_{t-1}^{-},\bar{\mu}_{t-1}^{+},\bar{\mu}_{t-1}^{-},...,\bar{\mu}_{t-1}^{-}]

(239)

1. When
l=j
, we have

\displaystyle[\alpha_{t}I+(1-\alpha_{t})\frac{\mathbbm{1}\mathbbm{1}^{T}}{k}]_{l,\cdot}\odot[\overline{\alpha}_{t-1}I+(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t-1})\frac{\mathbbm{1}\mathbbm{1}^{T}}{k}]_{j,\cdot}

\displaystyle=[\underbrace{\mu_{t}^{-}\cdot\bar{\mu}_{t-1}^{-},...,\mu_{t}^{-}\cdot\bar{\mu}_{t-1}^{-}}_{l-1\text{ terms}},\mu_{t}^{+}\cdot\bar{\mu}_{t-1}^{+},\mu_{t}^{-}\cdot\bar{\mu}_{t-1}^{-},...]

(240)

Besides,

\displaystyle[\overline{\alpha}_{t}I+(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})\frac{\mathbbm{1}\mathbbm{1}^{T}}{k}]_{l,j}=\bar{\mu}_{t}^{+}

(241)

Therefore, we have the explicit form for
q(\mathbf{v}_{t-1}|\mathbf{v}=l,\mathbf{v}_{0}=j)

\displaystyle q(\mathbf{v}_{t-1}|\mathbf{v}_{t}=l,\mathbf{v}_{0}=j)=[\frac{\mu_{t}^{-}\cdot\bar{\mu}_{t-1}^{-}}{\bar{\mu}_{t}^{+}},...,\frac{\mu_{t}^{-}\cdot\bar{\mu}_{t-1}^{-}}{\bar{\mu}_{t}^{+}},\frac{\mu_{t}^{+}\cdot\bar{\mu}_{t-1}^{+}}{\bar{\mu}_{t}^{+}},\frac{\mu_{t}^{-}\cdot\bar{\mu}_{t-1}^{-}}{\bar{\mu}_{t}^{+}},...,\frac{\mu_{t}^{-}\cdot\bar{\mu}_{t-1}^{-}}{\bar{\mu}_{t}^{+}}]

(242)

2. When
l\neq j
, we have

\displaystyle[\alpha_{t}I+(1-\alpha_{t})\frac{\mathbbm{1}\mathbbm{1}^{T}}{k}]_{l,\cdot}\odot[\overline{\alpha}_{t-1}I+(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t-1})\frac{\mathbbm{1}\mathbbm{1}^{T}}{k}]_{j,\cdot}

\displaystyle=[\underbrace{\mu_{t}^{-}\cdot\bar{\mu}_{t-1}^{-},...,\mu_{t}^{-}\cdot\bar{\mu}_{t-1}^{-}}_{l-1\text{ terms}},\mu_{t}^{+}\cdot\bar{\mu}_{t}^{-},\underbrace{...,\mu_{t}^{-}\cdot\bar{\mu}_{t-1}^{-},...}_{j-l-1\text{ terms}},\mu_{t}^{-}\cdot\bar{\mu}_{t-1}^{+},...,\mu_{t}^{-}\cdot\bar{\mu}_{t-1}^{-}]

(243)

\displaystyle[\overline{\alpha}_{t}I+(1-\overline{\alpha}_{t})\frac{\mathbbm{1}\mathbbm{1}^{T}}{k}]_{l,j}=\bar{\mu}_{t}^{-}

(244)

The explicit form of
q(\mathbf{v}_{t-1}|\mathbf{v}_{t}=l,\mathbf{v}_{0}=j)
is given as:

\displaystyle q(\mathbf{v}_{t-1}|\mathbf{v}_{t}=l,\mathbf{v}_{0}=j)=[\frac{\mu_{t}^{-}\cdot\bar{\mu}_{t-1}^{-}}{\bar{\mu}_{t}^{-}},...,\frac{\mu_{t}^{+}\cdot\bar{\mu}_{t-1}^{-}}{\bar{\mu}_{t}^{-}},...,\frac{\mu_{t}^{-}\cdot\bar{\mu}_{t-1}^{+}}{\bar{\mu}_{t}^{-}},...]

(245)

From above derivation, we can show that

\displaystyle\max_{\mathbf{v}_{0},\mathbf{v}_{t-1},\mathbf{v}_{t}}q(\mathbf{v}_{t-1}|\mathbf{v}_{t},\mathbf{v}_{0})=\frac{\mu_{t}^{+}\cdot\bar{\mu}_{t-1}^{+}}{\bar{\mu}_{t}^{+}},\min_{\mathbf{v}_{0},\mathbf{v}_{t-1},\mathbf{v}_{t}}q(\mathbf{v}_{t-1}|\mathbf{v}_{t},\mathbf{v}_{0})=\frac{\mu_{t}^{-}\cdot\bar{\mu}_{t-1}^{-}}{\bar{\mu}_{t}^{+}}.

(246)

∎

Lemma K.3
.

Given a positive bounded trivariate kernel
\mathcal{K}(x,\tilde{x},z)
such that
\mathcal{K}(x,\tilde{x},z)\in[c_{1},c_{2}]
and the conditional probabilities
\tilde{q}_{i}(\tilde{x}|z)
and
\tilde{p}_{i}(\tilde{x}|z)
satisfy

\displaystyle\mathcal{D}_{\text{KL}}(\tilde{q}_{i}(\tilde{x}|z)\|\tilde{p}_{i}(\tilde{x}|z))\leq\gamma,i\in\{0,1\},\forall z.

Define
q_{i}(x|z)
and
p_{i}(x|z)
as

\displaystyle q_{i}(x|z)=\int_{{\tilde{x}}}\mathcal{K}(x,\tilde{x},z)\tilde{q}_{i}(\tilde{x}|z)d\tilde{x},\quad p_{i}(x|z)=\int_{{\tilde{x}}}\mathcal{K}(x,\tilde{x},z)\tilde{p}_{i}(\tilde{x}|z)d\tilde{x},\quad i\in\{0,1\}.

(247)

we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{z}\left[\mathcal{D}_{\text{KL}}(p_{0}(x|z)\|p_{1}(x|z))\right]\leq\mathbb{E}_{z}\left[\mathcal{D}_{\text{KL}}(q_{0}(x|z)\|q_{1}(x|z))\right]+\frac{c_{2}^{2}\cdot k}{c_{1}}\sqrt{\frac{\gamma}{2}}

(248)

Proof of Lemma
K.3
.

From Pinsker’s inequality, we have

\displaystyle\int_{\tilde{x}}|\tilde{q}_{i}(\tilde{x}|z)-\tilde{p}_{i}(\tilde{x}|z)|d\tilde{x}\leq\sqrt{\frac{\mathcal{D}_{\text{KL}}(\tilde{q}_{i}(\tilde{x}|z)\|\tilde{p}_{i}(\tilde{x}|z))}{2}}\leq\sqrt{\frac{\gamma}{2}},\quad i\in\{0,1\}

(249)

Since positive kernel
\mathcal{K}(x,\tilde{x},z)\in[c_{1},c_{2}]
, we have

\displaystyle|q_{i}(x|z)-p_{i}(x|z)|=\int_{\tilde{x}}\mathcal{K}(x,\tilde{x},z)(\tilde{q}_{i}(\tilde{x}|z)-\tilde{p}_{i}(\tilde{x}|z))\leq c_{2}\sqrt{\frac{\gamma}{2}}

Thus,
\|q_{i}(x|z)-p_{i}(x|z)\|_{\text{TV}}\leq c_{2}k\sqrt{\frac{\gamma}{2}}
. Now, for any given
z
, we consider
\int_{x}p_{0}(x|z)\log\frac{p_{0}(x|z)}{p_{1}(x|z)}-\int_{x}q_{0}(x|z)\log\frac{q_{0}(x|z)}{q_{1}(x|z)}
:

\displaystyle\int_{x}p_{0}(x|z)\log\frac{p_{0}(x|z)}{p_{1}(x|z)}-\int_{x}q_{0}(x|z)\log\frac{q_{0}(x|z)}{q_{1}(x|z)}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\int\frac{(p_{0}(x|z)-q_{0}(x|z))(p_{0}(x|z)-p_{1}(x|z))}{p_{1}(x|z)}+\int q_{0}(x|z)(\log\frac{p_{0}(x|z)}{q_{0}(x|z)}+\log\frac{q_{1}(x|z)}{p_{1}(x|z)})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{c_{2}-c_{1}}{c_{1}}\int|p_{0}(x|z)-q_{0}(x|z)|+\int|p_{0}(x|z)-q_{0}(x|z)|+\frac{c_{2}}{c_{1}}\int|q_{1}(x|z)-p_{1}(x|z)|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{c_{2}}{c_{1}}(\int|p_{0}(x|z)-q_{0}(x|z)|+\int|p_{1}(x|z)-q_{1}(x|z)|)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{c_{2}^{2}\cdot k}{c_{1}}\sqrt{\frac{\gamma}{2}}

We take expectation on both sides and we obtain the result.
∎

Lemma K.4
.

If
\sum_{i}b_{i}=\sum_{i}a_{i}
, we have

\sum_{i}(b_{i}-a_{i})c_{i}\leq\frac{1}{2}\|b-a\|_{\ell_{1}}(\max c_{i}-\min c_{i})

Proof of Lemma
K.4
.

First, WLOG we can assume
b_{1}-a_{1}\geq b_{2}-a_{2}\geq...\geq b_{r}-a_{r}\geq 0\geq b_{r+1}-a_{r+1}\geq...\geq b_{n}-a_{n}
. Since
\sum_{i}b_{i}=\sum_{i}a_{i}
, we have
\sum_{j=1}^{r}(b_{j}-a_{j})=-\sum_{s=r+1}^{n}(b_{j}-a_{j})=\frac{1}{2}\|b-a\|_{l_{1}}
. Further, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{i}(b_{i}-a_{i})\cdot c_{i}=

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{r}(b_{j}-a_{j})\cdot c_{j}+\sum_{s=r+1}^{n}(b_{j}-a_{j})\cdot c_{s}

(250)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{r}(b_{j}-a_{j})\cdot c_{j}+\sum_{s=r+1}^{n}(-(b_{j}-a_{j}))\cdot(-c_{s})

(251)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\max c_{i}\cdot\frac{1}{2}\|b-a\|_{l_{1}}+(-\min c_{i})\cdot\frac{1}{2}\|b-a\|_{l_{1}}

(252)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\|b-a\|_{l_{1}}(\max c_{i}-\min c_{i})

(253)

∎

Lemma K.5
.

For
x,y,z\geq 0
,

\displaystyle\frac{(a+1)y+z}{(ax+y+z)(ay+x+z+1)}\leq\frac{a+1}{(a^{2}+1)x+ay+az+1}

(254)

Proof of Lemma
K.5
.

We reformulate the above inequality into a polynomial division problem. Define a multivariate linear function
f(x,y,z)=M_{1}x+M_{2}y+M_{3}z+M_{4}
such that

\displaystyle[(a+1)y+z]\cdot f(x,y,z)\leq(ax+y+z)\cdot(ay+x+z+1)

(255)

We expand the terms on both sides, we have

\displaystyle\text{R.H.S}-\text{L.H.S}

(256)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle[a^{2}-M_{1}(a+1)]xy+[a-M_{2}(a+1)]y^{2}+((a+1)-M_{2}-M_{3}(a+1))yz

\displaystyle+ax^{2}+xy+(a+1-M_{1})xz+(1-M_{3})z^{2}+ax+[1-M_{4}(a+1)]y+(1-M_{4})z

(257)

Let
M_{1}=\frac{a^{2}+1}{a=1},M_{2}=\frac{a}{a+1},M_{3}=\frac{a}{a+1}
and
M_{4}=\frac{1}{a+1}
, we further have

\displaystyle\text{R.H.S}-\text{L.H.S}=\frac{1}{a+1}yz+ax^{2}+\frac{2a}{a+1}xy+\frac{1}{a+1}z^{2}+ax+\frac{a}{a+1}\geq 0

(258)

Thus, we prove the inequality.
∎

Lemma K.6
.

We upper bound the total variation of conditional probability gap as follows:

•

If
\mathbf{v}_{t}^{i}=\mathbf{v}^{*i}
, we have

\displaystyle\|q(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|0}^{i}|\mathbf{v}_{t|0}=\mathbf{v}_{t})-q(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|1}^{i}|\mathbf{v}_{t|1}=\mathbf{v}_{t})\|_{l_{1}}\leq\frac{\mu_{t}^{+}\cdot\frac{\bar{\mu}_{t-1}^{+}}{\bar{\mu}_{t}^{+}}\cdot(1-\frac{\bar{R}_{t}}{\bar{R}_{t-1}})}{1+\zeta(\mathcal{V}_{1},\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{v}_{t},t)}

(259)

•

If
\mathbf{v}_{t}^{i}\neq\mathbf{v}^{*i}
, we have

\displaystyle\|q(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|0}^{i}|\mathbf{v}_{t|0}=\mathbf{v}_{t})-q(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|1}^{i}|\mathbf{v}_{t|1}=\mathbf{v}_{t})\|_{l_{1}}\leq\frac{\mu_{t}^{+}\cdot\frac{\bar{\mu}_{t-1}^{-}}{\bar{\mu}_{t}^{-}}\cdot(\frac{\bar{R}_{t-1}}{\bar{R}_{t}}-1)}{1+\zeta(\mathcal{V}_{1},\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{v}_{t},t)}

(260)

Proof of Lemma
K.6
.

1. When
\mathbf{v}_{t}^{i}=\mathbf{v}^{*i}
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\|q(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|0}^{i}|\mathbf{v}_{t|0}=\mathbf{v}_{t})-q(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|1}^{i}|\mathbf{v}_{t|1}=\mathbf{v}_{t})\|_{l_{1}}

(261)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i}}(q(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|0}^{i}|\mathbf{v}_{t|0}=\mathbf{v}_{t})-q(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|1}^{i}|\mathbf{v}_{t|1}=\mathbf{v}_{t}))_{+}

(262)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i}=1}^{k}\frac{\sum_{\mathbf{v}_{0}\in\mathcal{V}_{1}}\tau(\mathbf{v}^{*i},\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i},\mathbf{v}_{t}^{i})\cdot(1-\frac{\tau(\mathbf{v}_{0}^{i},\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i},\mathbf{v}_{t}^{i})}{\tau(\mathbf{v}^{*i},\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i},\mathbf{v}_{t}^{i})})_{+}(\bar{R}_{t})^{\bar{\omega}(\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{v}_{t})-\bar{\omega}(\mathbf{v}_{0},\mathbf{v}_{t})}}{(\sum_{\mathbf{v}_{0}\in\mathcal{V}_{1}}(\bar{R}_{t})^{\bar{\omega}(\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{v}_{t})-\bar{\omega}(\mathbf{v}_{0},\mathbf{v}_{t})})(1+\sum_{\mathbf{v}_{0}\in\mathcal{V}_{1}}(\bar{R}_{t})^{\bar{\omega}(\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{v}_{t})-\bar{\omega}(\mathbf{v}_{0},\mathbf{v}_{t})})}

(263)

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(i)}}{{=}}

\displaystyle\tau(\mathbf{v}^{*i},\mathbf{v}^{*i},\mathbf{v}_{t}^{i})\cdot\frac{\sum_{\mathbf{v}_{0}\in\mathcal{V}_{1}}(1-\frac{\tau(\mathbf{v}_{0}^{i},\mathbf{v}^{*i},\mathbf{v}_{t}^{i})}{\tau(\mathbf{v}^{*i},\mathbf{v}^{*i},\mathbf{v}_{t}^{i})})(\bar{R}_{t})^{\bar{\omega}(\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{v}_{t})-\bar{\omega}(\mathbf{v}_{0},\mathbf{v}_{t})}}{(\sum_{\mathbf{v}_{0}\in\mathcal{V}_{1}}(\bar{R}_{t})^{\bar{\omega}(\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{v}_{t})-\bar{\omega}(\mathbf{v}_{0},\mathbf{v}_{t})})(1+\sum_{\mathbf{v}_{0}\in\mathcal{V}_{1}}(\bar{R}_{t})^{\bar{\omega}(\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{v}_{t})-\bar{\omega}(\mathbf{v}_{0},\mathbf{v}_{t})})}

(264)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\mu_{t}^{+}\cdot\frac{\bar{\mu}_{t-1}^{+}}{\bar{\mu}_{t}^{+}}\cdot(1-\frac{\bar{R}_{t}}{\bar{R}_{t-1}})\cdot\sum_{\mathbf{v}_{0}\in\mathcal{V}_{1},\mathbf{v}_{0}^{i}\neq\mathbf{v}^{*i}}(\bar{R}_{t})^{\bar{\omega}(\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{v}_{t})-\bar{\omega}(\mathbf{v}_{0},\mathbf{v}_{t})}}{(\sum_{\mathbf{v}_{0}\in\mathcal{V}_{1}}(\bar{R}_{t})^{\bar{\omega}(\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{v}_{t})-\bar{\omega}(\mathbf{v}_{0},\mathbf{v}_{t})})(1+\sum_{\mathbf{v}_{0}\in\mathcal{V}_{1}}(\bar{R}_{t})^{\bar{\omega}(\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{v}_{t})-\bar{\omega}(\mathbf{v}_{0},\mathbf{v}_{t})})}

(265)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mu_{t}^{+}\cdot\frac{\bar{\mu}_{t-1}^{+}}{\bar{\mu}_{t}^{+}}\cdot(1-\frac{\bar{R}_{t}}{\bar{R}_{t-1}})\cdot\frac{1}{1+\zeta(\mathcal{V}_{1},\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{v}_{t},t)}

(266)

where
(i)
is from that
\tau(\mathbf{v}_{0}^{i},\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i},\mathbf{v}_{t}^{i})/\tau(\mathbf{v}^{*i},\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i},\mathbf{v}_{t}^{i})<1
if and only if
\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i}=\mathbf{v}^{*i}
.

2. When
\mathbf{v}_{t}^{i}\neq\mathbf{v}^{*i}
, to simplify the notation, we abbreviate the terms as:

\displaystyle\sum_{\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i}}|\sum_{\mathbf{v}_{0}\in\mathcal{V}_{1}}|:=\sum_{\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i}=1}^{k}\biggl|\sum_{\mathbf{v}_{0}\in\mathcal{V}_{1}}\frac{\tau(\mathbf{v}^{*i},\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i},\mathbf{v}_{t}^{i})\cdot(1-\frac{\tau(\mathbf{v}_{0}^{i},\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i},\mathbf{v}_{t}^{i})}{\tau(\mathbf{v}^{*i},\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i},\mathbf{v}_{t}^{i})})(\bar{R}_{t})^{\bar{\omega}(\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{v}_{t})-\bar{\omega}(\mathbf{v}_{0},\mathbf{v}_{t})}}{\zeta(\mathcal{V}_{1},\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{v}_{t},t)(1+\zeta(\mathcal{V}_{1},\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{v}_{t},t))}\biggl|

\displaystyle\sum_{\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i}=\mathbf{v}^{*i}}|\sum_{\mathbf{v}_{0}\in\mathcal{V}_{1}}|:=\sum_{\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i}=\mathbf{v}^{*i}}\biggl|\sum_{\mathbf{v}_{0}\in\mathcal{V}_{1}}\frac{\tau(\mathbf{v}^{*i},\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i},\mathbf{v}_{t}^{i})\cdot(1-\frac{\tau(\mathbf{v}_{0}^{i},\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i},\mathbf{v}_{t}^{i})}{\tau(\mathbf{v}^{*i},\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i},\mathbf{v}_{t}^{i})})(\bar{R}_{t})^{\bar{\omega}(\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{v}_{t})-\bar{\omega}(\mathbf{v}_{0},\mathbf{v}_{t})}}{\zeta(\mathcal{V}_{1},\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{v}_{t},t)(1+\zeta(\mathcal{V}_{1},\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{v}_{t},t))}\biggl|

\displaystyle\sum_{\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i}\neq\mathbf{v}^{*i},\mathbf{v}_{t}^{i}}|\sum_{\mathbf{v}_{0}\in\mathcal{V}_{1}}|:=\sum_{\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i}\neq\mathbf{v}^{*i},\mathbf{v}_{t}^{i}}\biggl|\sum_{\mathbf{v}_{0}\in\mathcal{V}_{1}}\frac{\tau(\mathbf{v}^{*i},\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i},\mathbf{v}_{t}^{i})\cdot(1-\frac{\tau(\mathbf{v}_{0}^{i},\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i},\mathbf{v}_{t}^{i})}{\tau(\mathbf{v}^{*i},\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i},\mathbf{v}_{t}^{i})})(\bar{R}_{t})^{\bar{\omega}(\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{v}_{t})-\bar{\omega}(\mathbf{v}_{0},\mathbf{v}_{t})}}{\zeta(\mathcal{V}_{1},\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{v}_{t},t)(1+\zeta(\mathcal{V}_{1},\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{v}_{t},t))}\biggl|

\displaystyle\sum_{\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i}}|\sum_{\mathbf{v}_{0}=\mathbf{v}_{t}^{i}}|:=\sum_{\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i}=1}^{k}\biggl|\sum_{\mathbf{v}_{0}\mathbf{v}_{t}^{i}}\frac{\tau(\mathbf{v}^{*i},\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i},\mathbf{v}_{t}^{i})\cdot(1-\frac{\tau(\mathbf{v}_{0}^{i},\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i},\mathbf{v}_{t}^{i})}{\tau(\mathbf{v}^{*i},\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i},\mathbf{v}_{t}^{i})})(\bar{R}_{t})^{\bar{\omega}(\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{v}_{t})-\bar{\omega}(\mathbf{v}_{0},\mathbf{v}_{t})}}{\zeta(\mathcal{V}_{1},\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{v}_{t},t)(1+\zeta(\mathcal{V}_{1},\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{v}_{t},t))}\biggl|

We have

\displaystyle\|q(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|0}^{i}|\mathbf{v}_{t|0}=\mathbf{v}_{t})-q(\mathbf{v}_{t-1|1}^{i}|\mathbf{v}_{t|1}=\mathbf{v}_{t})\|=\sum_{\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i}}|\sum_{\mathbf{v}_{0}\in\mathcal{V}_{1}}|

(267)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i}=\mathbf{v}^{*i}}|\sum_{\mathbf{v}_{0}\in\mathcal{V}_{1}}|+\sum_{\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i}\neq\mathbf{v}^{*i},\mathbf{v}_{t}^{i}}|\sum_{\mathbf{v}_{0}\neq\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i}}|+\sum_{\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i}\neq\mathbf{v}^{*i},\mathbf{v}_{t}^{i}}|\sum_{\mathbf{v}_{0}=\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i}}|+\sum_{\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{i}=\mathbf{v}_{t}^{i}}|\sum_{\mathbf{v}_{0}\in\mathcal{V}_{1}}|

(268)

\displaystyle=
